OFFENCES OF A RACIAL, REGIONALIST, RELIGIOUS, SEXIST OR CASTE NATURE

Article 322-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs and a residence ban of five years any act of discrimination, any manifestation contrary to freedom of conscience and freedom of worship likely to set persons against one another.

Article 322-2

Is considered an act of discrimination, any distinction, exclusion, restriction, or preference based on race, ethnicity, colour, sex, language, religion, political opinion or any other opinion, national or social origin, fortune, birth which has the purpose or effect of destroying or compromising the recognition, enjoyment or exercise, under conditions of equality, of human rights and fundamental freedoms in the political, economic, social and cultural fields or in any other field of public life.

Article 322-3

Discrimination is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, when it consists of:	refusing to provide a good or service;	hindering the normal exercise of any economic activity;	refusing to hire, sanctioning or dismissing a person;	making any service or benefit conditional upon a condition based on discrimination.

Article 322-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs any public speech or writing that justifies or purports to justify any discrimination as referred to in Article 322-2 above, any hatred, any intolerance or violence for any reason whatsoever towards a person or group of persons.
If such speeches or writings have resulted in violence against persons and/or destruction of property, the penalty is three years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.
The court seized may, in addition, order the confiscation of the media containing such writings or speeches.

Article 322-5

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs non-public incitement to discrimination as referred to in Article 322-2 above, to intolerance, hatred or violence for any reason whatsoever towards a person or group of persons.

CHAPITRE 3: CRIMES AND OFFENCES RELATING TO THE EXERCISE OF CIVIC RIGHTS

Article 323-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by unlawful assembly, assault or threat, prevents one or more persons from exercising their civic rights.

Article 323-2

The penalty is imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, if the acts are committed pursuant to a concerted plan to be executed either on the national territory or in one or more administrative entities.

CHAPITRE 4: ATTACKS ON LIBERTY

Article 324-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, any public agent or any other representative of authority who orders or causes to be ordered any arbitrary act or attack either on individual liberty, or on the civic rights of one or more persons, or on the texts in force. If they justify that they acted by lawful order of their superiors and within the limits of competence for matters within the jurisdiction of the latter, they are exempt from penalty, which, in this case, is applied only to the superiors who gave the order.

Article 324-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any minister who orders or commits the acts mentioned in Article 324-1 above and who refuses or neglects to cause such acts to cease.

Article 324-3

If the minister accused of having ordered or authorized the act contrary to the texts claims that the signature imputed to them was obtained by deception, they are required, while causing the act to cease, to denounce the person they declare to be the author of the deception, otherwise, they are prosecuted personally and are liable to the penalty provided for in Article 324-2 above.

Article 324-4

The offences provided for in Article 324-1 above may give rise to payment of damages which may not be less than ten thousand (10,000) CFA francs per day of unlawful detention and per person.

Article 324-5

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, if the act contrary to the texts in force is done using a false signature of the name of the minister or of a public official, the perpetrators of the forgery and those who knowingly make use of it.

Article 324-6

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any official or public agent responsible for administrative or judicial police who, having knowledge of facts of unlawful or arbitrary detention in any place, refuses or neglects to record them and to cause them to cease.

Article 324-7

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any person in charge of places of detention who:	receives a person without a warrant or judgment or without an order from the government in case of expulsion or extradition;	detains them or refuses to present them to the judicial police officer or to the bearer of their orders;	refuses to exhibit the registers to any authority responsible for inspecting them.

Article 324-8

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years:	public prosecutors, prosecutors of Faso, their deputies, judges or judicial police officers who detain or cause to be detained a person outside the places and outside the conditions determined by law;	public prosecutors, prosecutors of Faso, their deputies and judges who bring or try a person before a criminal court without them having been previously and lawfully prosecuted.

CHAPITRE 5: COALITION OF PUBLIC AGENTS

Article 325-1

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to five years, depositaries of public authority who, either by assembly of individuals or bodies, or by delegation or correspondence between them, concert measures contrary to the law.

Article 325-2

If by one of the means expressed above, measures are concerted against the execution of laws or against the orders of the government, the author or authors are punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years.
The court seized may in addition pronounce a residence ban for a duration which may not exceed five years.
If this concertation takes place between the civil authorities and the military or security corps or their leaders, the authors or instigators are punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years.
If the concertation had as its object or result a conspiracy threatening the security of the State, the authors are punished by imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years.

CHAPITRE 6

MALFEASANCE AND ENCROACHMENT BY ADMINISTRATIVE, LEGISLATIVE AND JUDICIAL AUTHORITIES

Article 326-1

Any crime committed by a public agent in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions is malfeasance.
Any act of malfeasance is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs when the law has not provided for a higher penalty.
Misdemeanors and petty offenses committed by the public agent in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions do not constitute malfeasance.

Article 326-2

Are punished for malfeasance by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs:	counselors, judges, prosecutors general, prosecutors of Faso, their deputies, judicial police officers who intentionally interfere in the exercise of legislative power either by regulations containing legislative provisions, or by stopping or suspending the execution of one or more laws, or by deliberating on the question of whether laws will be published or executed;	counselors, judges, prosecutors general, prosecutors of Faso, their deputies, judicial police officers who intentionally exceed their powers by interfering in matters attributed to administrative authorities, either by making regulations on these matters, or by prohibiting the execution of orders emanating from the administration or who, having permitted or ordered the summoning of administrators by reason of the exercise of their functions, persist in the execution of their judgments or orders notwithstanding the annulment that may have been pronounced or the conflict that may have been notified to them.

Article 326-3

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, ministers, mayors and all administrative authorities acting in their official capacity, who intentionally interfere in the exercise of legislative power or who adopt general texts tending to give orders or prohibitions of any kind to courts and tribunals.
Are also punished by the same penalties deputies or any other persons enjoying legislative power who intentionally interfere in the exercise of judicial power or who intentionally adopt general texts tending to give orders or prohibitions of any kind to courts or tribunals.

Article 326-4

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) francs, administrative authorities who encroach upon judicial functions or unduly assume jurisdiction over private rights and interests within the jurisdiction of the tribunals and who after the claim of the parties or one of them, nevertheless decide the matter before the authority responsible for settling the conflict has ruled.

TITRE III

CORRUPTION AND RELATED OFFENSES CHAPITRE 1: CORRUPTION OF PUBLIC AGENTS

Article 331-1

For the purposes of this title, the following terms mean: Public official:
• any civilian or military person who holds a legislative, executive, administrative or judicial mandate, whether appointed or elected, on a permanent or temporary basis, whether remunerated or not and regardless of their hierarchical level or seniority;
• any other civilian or military person vested with a function or mandate, even temporarily, remunerated or not, who contributes, in that capacity, to the service of a public body or public enterprise, or any other enterprise in which the State holds all or part of its capital, or any other enterprise that provides a public service;
• the agent of any legal person under private law responsible for the execution of a public service or public procurement regardless of the modalities of the mission entrusted to them;
• any other civilian or military person defined as a public official or assimilated thereto in accordance with the legislation and regulations in force;
foreign public official: any civilian or military person who holds a legislative, executive, administrative or judicial mandate with a foreign country, whether appointed or elected; and any civilian or military person who exercises a public function for a foreign country, including for a public body or public enterprise; enrichment balance sheet: the statement of movable and immovable acquisitions during a given period balanced against the legal income obtained or legal means mobilized that enabled these acquisitions; property: all types of assets, corporeal or incorporeal, movable or immovable, tangible or intangible, as well as legal instruments or documents attesting to the ownership of these assets or the rights relating thereto; gift: a present, an object given to a person with the intention of pleasing them; confiscation: the permanent deprivation of property by decision of a judicial body or any other competent authority; conflict of interest: any situation of interference between a public interest and public or private interests that is likely to influence or appear to influence the independent, impartial and objective exercise of a function; Convention: the United Nations Convention against Corruption; donation: the act of voluntarily transferring something to a person without asking for anything in exchange; entity: an organized set of corporeal or incorporeal elements or natural or legal persons, pursuing its own objective; official of a public international organization: any international official or any person authorized by such an organization to act on its behalf; freezing or seizure: the temporary prohibition of the transfer, conversion, disposition or movement of property, or the temporary assumption of custody or control of property by decision of a court or other competent authority;
conventional hospitality: any disinterested act generally accepted in social relations and falling within the customs of society; predicate offence: any offence as a result of which proceeds are generated that may become the subject of money laundering in accordance with the applicable legislation relating thereto; controlled delivery: the method of allowing the exit from the national territory, passage through the territory or entry into the territory of illicit or suspected illicit shipments, with the knowledge and under the control of the competent authorities, in order to investigate an offence and identify the persons involved in its commission; assets: all movable and immovable property, rights and claims belonging to the declarant and related persons but also the debts and financial commitments contracted by the latter; related person: any person related to the declarant by the bonds of marriage, de facto union, filiation or adoption, with the exception of adult children. proceeds of crime: any property derived, directly or indirectly, from the commission of an offence or obtained, directly or indirectly, by committing it. misuse of the single-source or direct agreement procedure: exceeding the quota set by WAEMU regulations and awarding more than one contract by the single-source or direct agreement procedure either to a natural or legal person, or to close relatives up to the 4th degree in the direct line and the 5th in the collateral line in the same year.

Article 331-2

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine equal to twice the value of the promises accepted, undue advantages or things received or requested, provided that said fine is not less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs: anyone who promises, offers or grants to a public official, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, either for himself or for another

person or entity, so that he performs or refrains from performing an act in the exercise of his functions; any public official who solicits or accepts, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, for himself or for another person or entity, in order to perform or refrain from performing an act within his functions.

CHAPTER 2: RELATED OFFENCES

Section 1: Unjustified advantages and corruption in public procurement

Article 332-1

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs: any public official who enters into, endorses or revises a contract, agreement, public procurement or amendment in violation of the legislative and regulatory provisions in force in order to procure an unjustified advantage for another; any merchant, industrialist, artist or artisan, private sector entrepreneur or in general, any natural or legal person who enters into, even occasionally, a contract or public procurement with the State, territorial authorities, public law establishments or bodies and State companies by taking advantage of the authority or influence of the agents of the aforementioned bodies to increase the prices they normally and habitually charge or to modify, to their advantage, the quality of goods or services or the delivery or supply deadlines.

Article 332-2

Is punishable by the penalties provided for in Article 332-1 above, anyone who compels or attempts to compel by force or threats, corrupts or attempts to corrupt by promise, offer, donation or present, a public official, whether or not the attempt was followed by effect.

Article 332-3

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine equivalent to three times the amount or value of the advantage received or to be received, any public official who, on the occasion of the preparation, negotiation, conclusion or execution of a public procurement, contract or amendment concluded in the name of the State or territorial authorities, public establishments of the State or State companies, receives or attempts to receive, directly or indirectly, for his benefit or for the benefit of a third party, remuneration or an advantage of any nature whatsoever from a private contractor.

Article 332-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine equivalent to twice the promises accepted, undue advantages, things received or requested of the value of a public contract and a prohibition from bidding on public contracts for two years, any natural or legal person contracting with the State or local authorities, public establishments of the State or State-owned companies who grants or proposes remuneration or any advantage by himself or through an intermediary to a public official with a view to obtaining a public contract.

Article 332-5

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine equivalent to twice the promises accepted, undue advantages, things received or requested, of the value of the public contract, any public official who abusively resorts to the direct agreement procedure in a public contract concluded in the name of the State or local authorities, public establishments of the State or State-owned companies.

Section 2

Corruption of foreign public officials and officials of public international organizations

Article 332-6

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs: anyone who promises, offers or grants to a foreign public official or an official of a public international organization, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, for himself or for another person or entity, so that he performs or refrains from performing an act in the exercise of his functions, with a view to obtaining or retaining a public contract or an undue advantage in connection with international trade or otherwise; any foreign public official or official of a public international organization who solicits or accepts, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, for himself or for another person or entity, in order to perform or refrain from performing an act within his functions.

Section 3: Misappropriation of property by a public official Article 332-7

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs any public official who misappropriates, destroys or dissipates for his own benefit or for the benefit of another person or entity, any property, any funds or securities, public or private, or anything of value that have been entrusted to him either by virtue of a contract or by reason of his functions.

Section 4

Unlawful and abusive use and retention of public property by a public official

Article 332-8

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) francs

CFA, any public official who knowingly and unduly retains for his own benefit or for the benefit of another person or entity, any property, any public funds or securities, or anything of value that have been entrusted to him either by virtue of a contract or by reason of his functions, or who makes unlawful and abusive use of public property.

Section 5: Extortion Article 332-9

Any public official who solicits, receives, demands or orders the collection of what he knows is not due or exceeds what is due, either to himself, or to the administration, or to the parties for whom he collects, is punished: by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine equal to twice the proceeds of the offense if the value is less than or equal to five hundred thousand (500,000) francs, provided that this fine may not be less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs; by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine equal to twice the value of the proceeds of the offense if the latter exceeds five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs provided that this fine may not be less than ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Section 6: Unlawful exemptions and franchises Article 332-10

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two to ten times the value of the property or right compromised, any agent of the State who, for whatever reason, unlawfully grants exemptions or franchises from taxes, duties, fines, bonds and other rights or gives free of charge or sells at a low price, public property in violation of laws and regulations.

Section 7: Influence peddling and abuse of function Article 332-11

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs: anyone who promises, offers or grants to a public official or any other person, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, so that said official or said person uses his real or supposed influence with a view to obtaining from an administration or a public authority, an undue advantage for the initial instigator of the act or for any other person; any public official or any other person who solicits, accepts directly or indirectly, an undue advantage for himself or for another person, in order to use his real or supposed influence with a view to obtaining from an administration or a public authority an undue advantage.

Article 332-12

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the act, for a public official, of intentionally abusing his functions or his position, by performing or refraining from performing, in the exercise of his functions, an act in violation of laws and regulations in order to obtain an undue advantage for himself or for another person or entity.

Section 8: Overbilling Article 332-13

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three years to five years, confiscation of the amount received as a rebate and a fine equivalent to three times the value received provided that this fine may not be less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the sanctions provided for by the regulations on public contracts, any public official who proceeds or causes to proceed with the billing, for an amount higher than its actual cost, of a good or service to be acquired by an entity of the national or local public administration.

Section 9: Nepotism and favoritism Article 332-14

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to three years and prohibition from exercising a function in the national public administration for a period of five years, any political figure, any public official, any member of the defense and security forces who uses his function or uses his influence, to procure directly or indirectly any undue material advantage, or employment, to a member of his family in the direct line or in the collateral line up to the third degree.

Article 332-15

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any official of the administrative or judicial order, any military person or equivalent, any agent or representative of the administration, any person vested with an elective mandate, who procures or attempts to procure for another an unjustified advantage by an act contrary to legislative or regulatory provisions intended to guarantee equal access and equality of candidates in public employment and public contracts.

Section 10: Incompatible trade Article 332-16

Any public agent engaging in commercial or lucrative activities other than the marketing of their non-industrial agro-pastoral, literary, scientific and artistic productions is punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.
In addition, confiscation of the means of such trade or lucrative activity shall be ordered.

Section 11: Misappropriation of public property Article 332-17

Anyone who misappropriates or dissipates for personal purposes public funds, active effects in lieu thereof, payment instruments, securities, acts containing or creating obligations or discharge, equipment or movable objects belonging to, intended for or entrusted to the State, local authorities or public institutions, organizations or companies benefiting from State participation, which they hold by reason of their functions, is guilty of misappropriation of public property:	if the value of the misappropriation is less than or equal to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the penalty is imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine equal to three times the value of the misappropriated property without such fine being less than one million (1,000,000) CFA francs;	if the value is greater than one million (1,000,000) CFA francs and less than or equal to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the penalty is imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine equal to three times the value of the misappropriated property without such fine being less than five million (5,000,000) CFA francs;	if the value is greater than ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the penalty is imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine equal to three times the value of the misappropriated property without such fine being less than ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.
In the cases provided for in bullets 2 and 3 of this article, the court may also order the prohibition from exercising civil rights for a period not exceeding five years.

Section 12: Conflict of interest and unlawful taking of interest Article 332-18

Any public agent whose private interests coincide with the public interest and are

likely to influence the normal exercise of their functions, who does not inform their hierarchical superior, is punished by imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 332-19

Any public agent who, either directly, indirectly or by simulated act, takes, receives or retains any interest whatsoever in acts, awards, tenders, enterprises of which they have, at the time of the act in whole or in part, the administration or supervision or, who, having the mission to order payment or to settle an affair, has taken any interest therein, is punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 332-20

Any public agent responsible by reason of their function for:	the supervision and control of a private enterprise;	the conclusion, on behalf of the State or public authorities, of contracts or agreements of any nature or with a private enterprise;	the expression of opinions on contracts or agreements of any nature with a private enterprise, who, during a period of five years from the cessation of the function, exercises a corporate mandate or remunerated activity, except with express authorization from the competent administrative authority, or takes or receives a capital participation, except by hereditary devolution, either in any of the enterprises referred to above, or in any enterprise possessing with one of them at least thirty percent common capital, or in any enterprise having concluded with one of them a contract involving exclusivity in law or in fact, is punished by imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 332-21

The provisions of articles 332-19 and 332-20 above also apply to agents of public institutions, nationalized enterprises and mixed economy companies in which the State or public authorities

hold directly or indirectly at least twenty-five percent of the capital.

Section 13: Unlawful simulation Article 332-22

Any person from the private sector who agrees to represent a public agent, acquires property or engages in commercial or lucrative activities on their behalf pursuant to a written or verbal nominee agreement is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.
The public agent, party to such nominee agreement, is punished by the same penalties, notwithstanding the administrative and disciplinary sanctions to which they may be subject.
In addition, the court orders ex officio the confiscation of the property and income that are the subject of the offense.

Section 14: Offense of appearance Article 332-23

Anyone who cannot reasonably justify the increase in their standard of living beyond a threshold set by regulation in relation to their lawful income is punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to twenty-five million (25,000,000) CFA francs.
The trial court orders the confiscation of the unjustified portion of the assets.
Anyone who has knowingly contributed by any means whatsoever to concealing the illicit nature of the property at the origin of the standard of living referred to in this article incurs the same penalty prescribed for the offense of receiving provided for in article 334-2 below.
The offense of appearance, as referred to, is a continuing offense characterized by the possession of illicit property or their use in a direct or indirect manner.

Section 15: Illicit enrichment Article 332-24

Anyone who has enriched themselves by using funds, equipment, instruments, acts, objects, effects or any other means belonging to the State is punished by the penalties of imprisonment provided for in article 332-17.

Section 16: Insider trading offense Article 332-25

Any agent of the public or private sector who exploits, in anticipation, knowingly, information not known to the public of a nature to break equality of opportunity or which would influence the course of any economic activity and of which they became aware by virtue of their situation or position is punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs which may go up to ten times the value of the profit realized.
The court orders ex officio the confiscation of the property and income that are the subject of the offense.

Section 17: Failure to declare or false declaration of interest or assets

Article 332-26

Any public agent legally subject to a declaration of interest and assets who, two months after a written formal notice from the institution responsible for combating corruption, knowingly, does not make a declaration of their assets or makes an incomplete, inaccurate or false declaration or knowingly formulates false observations or deliberately violates the obligations imposed upon them by law is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Section 18: Disclosure of information Article 332-27

Any agent of the authority holding declarations of interest and assets or of the registry offices found guilty of having disclosed, except in cases provided for by law, in any manner whatsoever, totally or partially, declarations of interest and assets or observations received, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Section 19: Offence of acceptance of undue gifts Article 332-28

Any public agent who accepts from a person a gift or any undue advantage likely to influence the handling of a procedure or transaction in progress related to his or her duties or having a connection with such handling or transaction, is punished by imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.
The donor is punished by the same penalties.

Section 20: Secret financing of political parties Article 332-29

Any political party official who receives secret financing for the benefit of his or her party is punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the criminal provisions in force relating to the financing of political parties.
Any person who secretly finances a political party is punished by the same penalties.

CHAPTER 3

CORRUPTION AND MISAPPROPRIATION OF PROPERTY IN THE PRIVATE SECTOR, PROHIBITED EMPLOYMENT

Article 333-1

The following are punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine equal to three times the value of the advantage or thing promised, offered or granted without being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs: anyone who promises, offers or grants, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage to any person who directs a private sector entity or works for such an entity, in any capacity whatsoever, for himself or herself or for another person, in order that he or she perform or refrain from performing an act in violation of his or her duties; any person directing a private sector entity or working for such an entity, in any capacity whatsoever, who solicits or accepts, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, for himself or herself or for another person or entity in order that he or she perform or refrain from performing an act in violation of his or her duties.

Article 333-2

Any person who directs a private sector entity or works for such an entity, in any capacity whatsoever, and who, intentionally, in the context of economic, financial or commercial activities, misappropriates any property or any private funds or securities or any other thing of value entrusted to him or her by reason of his or her duties, is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine equivalent to three times the value of the property or funds misappropriated without being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 333-3

Any public agent, entrusted by the State with a mission of control, supervision, administration or advice of a private enterprise, who exercises less than five years after cessation of his or her duties, a corporate office or remunerated

activity in that enterprise except with express authorization from the competent administrative authority, is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.

CHAPTER 4

LAUNDERING OF PROCEEDS OF CRIME AND RECEIVING PROCEEDS OF CORRUPTION AND RELATED OFFENCES

Article 334-1

Laundering of the proceeds of crimes provided for in this chapter is punished by the penalties provided for by the legislation on laundering.

Article 334-2

Any person who, knowingly, receives in whole or in part, the proceeds obtained by means of one of the offences provided for in this title, is punished by imprisonment of three years to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

CHAPTER 5: MISCELLANEOUS PROVISIONS

Section 1: Rules relating to penalties and limitation Article 335-1

If the perpetrator of one or more offences provided for in this title is a magistrate, civil servant exercising a senior function of the State, public officer, member of the superior authority responsible for combating corruption, officer, judicial police officer or having judicial police prerogatives or registry clerk, he or she incurs imprisonment of five years to ten years together with the fine provided for the offence committed.

Article 335-2

Any person who is the perpetrator or accomplice of one or more offences provided for in this chapter, who, before any prosecution, reveals an offence to the administrative or judicial authorities or to the relevant bodies and enables the identification of the persons implicated, benefits from an absolute excuse under the conditions provided for by this criminal code.

Except in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the maximum penalty incurred by any person who is the perpetrator or accomplice of one of the offences provided for in this chapter, who, after the commencement of prosecution, facilitates the arrest of one or more other persons involved, is reduced by half.

Article 335-3

In the event of conviction for one or more offences provided for in this title, the court may impose one or more of the following additional penalties: prohibition from exercising civil rights for a period that may not exceed five years; prohibition from exercising public functions or employment for a period that may not exceed five years; prohibition from obtaining any distinction or decoration awarded by the State or its subdivisions.

Article 335-4

Illicit revenues and property arising from one or more offences provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code may be seized or frozen by court decision or order of the competent authority.
In the event of conviction, the court orders, subject to cases of restitution of assets or the rights of third parties in good faith, the confiscation of illicit revenues and property for the benefit of the Public Treasury.
The court further orders the confiscation of misappropriated property or the value of the interest or gain obtained, even if such property has already been transferred to the ascendants, descendants, collaterals, spouse and relatives by marriage of the convicted person, whether they have remained in their state or converted into any other value.

Article 335-5

Public prosecution and penalties relating to the offences provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code are not subject to limitation when the proceeds of crime are transferred outside the national territory.

Section 2: Provisions relating to denunciations Article 335-6

Any person who, by virtue of his or her function or profession, permanent or temporary, has knowledge of one or more offences provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code, and does not inform the competent public authorities in time, is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.
However, any person who brings the information to the attention of private anti-corruption organizations benefits from an absolute excuse.

Article 335-7

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who has knowingly, and by any means whatsoever, made a slanderous or abusive denunciation of the offences provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code, to the competent authorities, against one or more persons.

Section 3 : Protection of witnesses, experts, whistleblowers and victims

Article 335-8

No person may be excluded from a recruitment procedure or from access to an internship or a training period in a company, no employee may be sanctioned, dismissed or subjected to discriminatory measures, direct or indirect, particularly with regard to remuneration, training, reclassification, assignment, qualification, classification, professional promotion, transfer or contract renewal for having reported or testified, either to their employer or to the judicial or administrative authorities, about one of the offences provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code of which they became aware in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions.

Article 335-9

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any person who resorts to physical or moral violence, revenge, intimidation or threat in any form whatsoever and in any manner whatsoever, against the person of witnesses, experts, whistleblowers or victims or their relatives or other persons close to them.
Is punished by the same penalties, any person who reveals the identity or address of a witness who has benefited from the protection provisions provided for by article 335-8 above.

CHAPITRE 6 : ELECTORAL FRAUD

Article 336-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs anyone who registers under a false name or false capacity or by registering has concealed an incapacity provided for by law or fraudulently registers on more than one list.
Is punished by the same penalties, anyone who obtains a false certificate of registration or removal from the electoral rolls. Is punished by the same penalties, anyone who has contravened the provisions relating to sponsorship.

Article 336-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one to three months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs anyone who, deprived of the right to vote, either following a judicial conviction or following a bankruptcy not followed by rehabilitation, has voted either by virtue of a registration on the lists prior to their deprivation, or by virtue of a subsequent registration, carried out with their complicity.

Article 336-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who has voted during an electoral consultation, either by virtue of a registration obtained in the cases provided for by article 336-1 above, or by falsely taking the names and capacities of a registered voter.

Article 336-4

Is punished by the penalties provided for in article 336-1 above, any citizen who has taken advantage of multiple registration to vote more than once.
The same penalties are applied to anyone who has prevented, by wilful non-observance of the law, the registration on an electoral list of a citizen fulfilling the conditions set by the electoral law.

Article 336-5

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs and deprivation of the right to vote and to be eligible, for at least five years and at most ten years, anyone who, being responsible in a ballot for receiving, counting or examining the ballots containing the votes of citizens, has removed, added or altered ballots, or has deliberately read a name other than that written.
Any failure to comply with one of the obligations prescribed in articles 86 and 96 of the law establishing the electoral code by a member of the polling station is equivalent to a violation of the ballot and subject to the sanctions provided for in paragraph 1 of this article.

Article 336-6

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs and deprivation of the right to vote and to be eligible for at least five years and at most ten years, anyone who, by gatherings, clamours or threatening demonstrations, has disrupted the operations of an electoral consultation, infringed upon the exercise of the electoral right or the freedom of the vote.

Article 336-7

Is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who participates in an electoral consultation with a concealed weapon.
The penalty is imprisonment from one to three months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, when the weapon is visible.

Article 336-8

Notwithstanding the provisions of this code regarding defamation and insult, any candidate or activist of political parties or formations who uses defamation, insults or any act of provocation that may taint the morality and serenity of the electoral campaign is punished by two or both of the following penalties:	imprisonment from one month to one year;	a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs;	deprivation of civil rights for at least two years and at most five years.

Article 336-9

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who breaks into a polling station, when the break-in is completed or attempted with violence.
If the perpetrators are carrying weapons, or if the ballot has been violated, the penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.

Article 336-10

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, present at the voting place, has committed acts of violence,

threats or behaviour likely to disturb public order and tranquillity, to delay or prevent electoral operations.
If the ballot has been violated, the term of imprisonment is from one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.
The penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, in cases where the offences provided for in paragraph 1 have been committed pursuant to a concerted plan to be executed in one or more electoral constituencies.

Article 336-11

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who removes a ballot box containing the votes cast and not yet counted.
If this removal was carried out by a group, with or without violence, the term of imprisonment is from five years to ten years and the fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 336-12

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, the violation of the ballot, either by members of the polling station, or by agents of the authority, assigned to guard the ballots not yet counted.

Article 336-13

The conviction, if it is pronounced, cannot, in any case, have the effect of annulling the election declared valid by the competent authorities or become final, by the absence of any regular protest formulated within the time limits provided by the laws in force.

Article 336-14

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to two years, and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever, either by assault, violence or threats against a voter, or by making him fear losing his employment or exposing his person, his family or his fortune to damage, has determined him or has attempted to determine him to abstain from voting or has influenced his vote.

Article 336-15

Outside the cases specifically provided for by the provisions of the laws and regulations in force, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever, either in an administrative commission, or in a polling station or in the offices of town halls, prefectures or outside of these, before during or after the ballot, has, by any fraudulent acts, violated or attempted to undermine the sincerity, prevented or attempted to prevent the operations of the ballot or who has changed or attempted to change the result.
The perpetrator may in addition be deprived of his civil rights for at least two years and at most five years.

Article 336-16

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to three months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever has distributed or caused to be distributed on the day of the ballot voting papers and other electoral campaign documents.
The court seized, orders, in addition, the confiscation of voting papers and all other electoral campaign documents.

Article 336-17

The public action and the civil action brought pursuant to articles 336-1 to 336-19 of this law are prescribed after six months, from the day of the proclamation of the result of the election.

Article 336-18

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to two years, a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs and the deprivation of civil rights for five years whoever is guilty of acts qualified as corruption or electoral fraud notably by:	fraudulent registration on the electoral rolls;	alteration of indelible ink in order to vote several times;	transfer and transport of voters to register on an electoral roll or to vote;	transfer of populations from one polling station to another;	lack of transparency in the use of resources that the State makes available to political parties for electoral campaigns;	falsification of electoral results.
Is punished by the same penalties whoever offers to electoral actors notably members of polling stations or scrutineers promises, gifts or advantages of any nature whatsoever.

Article 336-19

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs whoever proceeds to:	the use of attributes, goods or means of the State, like those of a public legal entity, an institution or a public body notably a company, an office, a State project and an international institution for electoral purposes;	advertising practices of a political nature;	media coverage of any disguised electoral campaign;	the distribution of leaflets, the posting of posters or the accomplishment of any act of electoral propaganda outside the places and time reserved for this propaganda.

TITRE IV

DE L'ABUS D'AUTORITÉ, DES ENTRAVES À L'EXERCICE DE LA JUSTICE ET DES DÉLITS RELATIFS À LA TENUE DE L'ÉTAT CIVIL

CHAPITRE 1 : DES ATTEINTES A L'AUTORITE DE LA JUSTICE

Section 1 : De l'entrave au bon fonctionnement de la justice Article 341-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs:	whoever resorts to physical force, threats or intimidation or promises, offers or grants an undue advantage to obtain false testimony or prevent testimony or the presentation of evidence in a proceeding in connection with the offences established in accordance with chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code;	whoever resorts to physical force, threats or intimidation to obstruct the course of investigations in connection with the commission of offences established in accordance with chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code.

Section 2

De la protection des témoins, des experts, des dénonciateurs et des victimes

Article 341-2

No person may be excluded from a recruitment procedure or from access to an internship or a training period in a company, no employee may be sanctioned, dismissed or be the subject of a discriminatory measure, direct or indirect, notably in matters of remuneration, training, reclassification, assignment, qualification, classification, professional promotion, transfer or contract renewal for having reported or testified, either to his employer, or to judicial or administrative authorities, of facts qualified as an offence within the meaning of this chapter of which he would have had knowledge in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions.

Article 341-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, whoever resorts to physical or moral violence, vengeance, intimidation or threat in any form whatsoever and in any manner whatsoever, against the person of witnesses, experts, whistleblowers or victims or their relatives or other persons who are close to them.
Is punished by the same penalties, whoever reveals the identity or address of a witness having benefited from the protection provisions provided for by this chapter.

Section 3

De la dénonciation calomnieuse ou abusive de corruption ou d'actes de corruption

Article 341-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, whoever has knowingly, and by any means whatsoever, made a slanderous or abusive denunciation of the offences provided for by this chapter, to the competent authorities, against one or more persons.

Section 4 : De la non dénonciation des infractions Article 341-5

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, by virtue of his function or profession, permanent or temporary, has knowledge of one or more offences provided for in this chapter, and does not inform the competent public authorities in time.
However, benefits from an absolute excuse the person who, for any reasons whatsoever, brings the information to the knowledge of private anti-corruption organizations.

Section 5: Abuse of authority and other obstructions to the exercise of justice

Article 341-6

The act, by a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission, acting in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions or mission, of entering or attempting to enter the home of another against their will outside the cases provided for by law, is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

Article 341-7

Any civil servant, public officer, administrator, agent or employee of the administration or police, any executor of judicial warrants or judgments, any commander in chief or subordinate commander of the public force who, without legitimate reason, uses or causes to be used violence against persons in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions, is punished according to the nature and gravity of such violence following the rule set out in Article 344-1 of this law.

Article 341-8

Any suppression, any opening of letters or parcels entrusted to the postal service, committed or facilitated by a civil servant or agent assigned to the postal administration, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to ten years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.
The court shall further pronounce against the convicted person a prohibition from any public employment or function for a period that may not exceed five years.

Article 341-9

The act, by a magistrate or any other person sitting in a judicial formation, of denying to

render justice after having been requested to do so and persisting in such denial after warning or injunction from their superiors, is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs and prohibition from the exercise of public functions for a period of two months to five years.

Article 341-10

A witness who alleges an excuse recognized as false is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to three months, in addition to fines pronounced for non-appearance.

Article 341-11

The act, by any person, of using threats, violence or committing any other act of intimidation to obtain from a magistrate, any person sitting in a judicial formation or participating in the public service of justice, or an agent of detection or prosecution services for offences in a foreign State or in an international court, that they perform or refrain from performing an act of their function, their mission or facilitated by their function or mission, is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to ten years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 341-12

Any civil servant, agent or employee of the administration who requests or orders, causes to be requested or ordered the action or use of public force against the execution of an order, a judicial warrant or any other order emanating from legitimate authority, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years.

Article 341-13

If as a result of said orders or requisitions, acts punishable by penalties stronger than those referred to in the preceding articles occur, these stronger penalties are applied to the civil servants, agents or employees guilty of having given said orders or made said requisitions.

Article 341-14

Any commander of internal security forces legally served with a requisition

from the authorized authority who refuses their services or refrains from causing the forces under their command to act, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to two years.

Section 6: Obstructions to judicial measures for the protection of minors

Article 341-15

If an incident relating to supervised liberty reveals a characterized lack of supervision on the part of the parents or guardian or custodian or systematic obstructions to the exercise of the supervision mission, the children's judge establishes an incident report which they send to the prosecutor of Faso, who refers the matter to the competent court.
Any person who commits the offence provided for in paragraph 1 of this article is punished by a term of one month to two months imprisonment and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 341-16

If on the occasion of the execution of a measure taken by a juvenile court in application of paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 of Article 116 of Law No. 015-2014/AN of 13 May 2014 on the protection of children in conflict with the law or in danger, an incident reveals a characterized lack of supervision on the part of the parents, legal representatives of the child or systematic obstructions to the exercise of the social worker's mission, the children's judge establishes an incident report which they send to the prosecutor of Faso, who refers the matter to the competent court.
Any person who commits the offence provided for in the first paragraph of this article is punished by a term of one month to two months imprisonment and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 341-17

If on the occasion of the execution of a measure taken by a juvenile court in application of Article 120 of Law No. 015-2014/AN of 13 May 2014 on the protection of children in conflict with the law or in danger, an incident reveals a characterized lack of supervision on the part of the parents, legal representatives or systematic obstructions to the exercise of the mission

of the social worker responsible for child protection, the children's judge establishes an incident report which they send to the prosecutor of Faso, who refers the matter to the competent court.
Any person who commits the offence provided for in paragraph 1 of this article is punished by a term of one month to two months imprisonment and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

CHAPTER 2

OFFENCES RELATING TO THE KEEPING OF CIVIL STATUS RECORDS AND TO ASSOCIATIONS

Article 342-1

The civil status officer or the person delegated by them by virtue of legal provisions, who records these acts elsewhere than in the register intended for that purpose or who omits to record them therein, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

Article 342-2

The civil status officer or the person delegated by them by virtue of legal provisions, who celebrates a marriage in violation of the conditions prescribed by law, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

Article 342-3

The provisions of Articles 342-1 and 342-2 above are applicable even where the nullity of the civil status records has not been requested or has been cured.

Article 342-4

Any person who contravenes the legislative and regulatory provisions on the conditions for the formation and declaration of associations is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs and in case of recidivism by a double fine.
The founders or leaders of associations who maintain themselves or who reconstitute them illegally after publication of the regulatory text of dissolution, as well as persons who, by propaganda, speeches, writings or by any other means, perpetuate or attempt to perpetuate the dissolved association, are punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand

(1,500,000) CFA francs.

Article 342-5

Any person who, in any capacity whatsoever, assumes or continues to assume the administration of an association notwithstanding the refusal or withdrawal of the declaration receipt or recognition of public utility or the finding of nullity, is punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 342-6

Any person who does not comply with the injunctions of the competent authority aimed at the recognition of an association or who gives false information, assumes or continues to assume the administration of foreign associations or establishments operating without authorization, is punished by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.
The same penalties apply to the leaders and participants in the activity of associations or establishments that operate without observing the conditions imposed by the authorization text or beyond the duration fixed by the latter.

CHAPITRE 3 : DE L'EXERCICE ILLEGAL DE L'AUTORITE PUBLIQUE

Article 343-1

Any official subject to oath who enters into the exercise of his functions without having taken the oath is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 343-2

Any person invested with an administrative mandate, or any public official dismissed, removed from office, suspended, legally prohibited who, after having had official knowledge thereof, continues the exercise of his functions or who, invested with elective or temporary functions, exercises them after having been replaced or after his functions have ended, is punished by imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.
The convicted person is further prohibited from any public employment or function for a period that may not exceed five years, all without prejudice to the heavier penalties imposed against the commanders referred to in Article 313-5 of this law.

CHAPITRE 4 : DISPOSITION PARTICULIERE

Article 344-1

Except in cases where the law specifically regulates the penalties incurred for crimes and offences committed by public or military officials or officers, those among them who participate in other crimes or offences that they were responsible for monitoring or suppressing are punished as follows:	by the maximum penalty, if it is an offence;	by imprisonment of eleven to thirty years, if the crime carries against any other perpetrator the penalty of imprisonment of more than ten years.

TITRE V : DES CRIMES ET DELITS COMMIS PAR LES PARTICULIERS CONTRE L'ORDRE PUBLIC

CHAPITRE 1 : DES ATTEINTES AU DRAPEAU ET AUX SYMBOLES DE LA NATION

Article 351-1

The act, during a demonstration organized or regulated by public authority, of publicly insulting the national anthem or the national flag is punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.
The penalty is imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, when the insult is committed in a gathering.

Article 351-2

Except in the cases provided for by Article 351-1 above, the following acts, when committed under conditions likely to disturb public order and with the intention of insulting the national flag, are punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs:	destroying it, damaging it or using it in a degrading manner, in a public place or place open to the public;	for the perpetrator of such acts, even committed in a private place, disseminating or causing to be disseminated the recording of images relating to their commission.
In case of recidivism, the penalty is imprisonment of four months to four years and a fine that may not be less than double the fine imposed upon the first conviction.

CHAPITRE 2 : DES OUTRAGES CONTRE LES DEPOSITAIRES DE L'AUTORITE PUBLIQUE

Article 352-1

Insult by gestures, threats, sending of objects, drawings, writings, words or cries uttered against the Head of State or a foreign Head of State is punished by imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.
When the insult is uttered against a president of an Institution, a member of the government, of a foreign government, a deputy or a diplomatic agent, the penalty is imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 352-2

Insult by words, gestures or threats, by writings or images of any nature not made public or by sending any objects addressed to a magistrate, an assessor or any person sitting in a judicial formation in the exercise of his functions or on the occasion of that exercise and tending to harm his honour, his dignity or the respect due to the function with which he is invested, is punished by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.
If the insult takes place at a hearing of a court, a tribunal or a judicial formation, the penalty of imprisonment is increased to one year to ten years and the fine to five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.
When the insult is perpetrated before the perpetrator had knowledge of the quality of the magistrate, the penalty of imprisonment is three months to one year and the fine two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

Article 352-3

The act of seeking to bring discredit, publicly by acts, words, writings or images of any nature, upon a judicial act or decision, under conditions likely to harm the authority of justice or its independence, is punished by imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.
The same penalties apply to the deliberate refusal to execute a judicial decision.
The provisions of paragraph 1 do not apply to acts, words, writings or images of any nature aimed at the reform, cassation or revision of a decision, nor to technical commentaries.
When the offences provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this article are committed by lawyers, notaries, bailiffs, court clerks or other auxiliaries of justice, the penalty of imprisonment is six months to two years.

When the offence is committed through the written or audiovisual press, the particular provisions of the laws governing these matters apply with regard to the determination of the persons responsible.

Article 352-4

Insult by words, gestures, threats, writings or drawings not made public or by sending any objects, tending to harm their honour or propriety and targeting any ministerial officer or any commander or agent of the public force, any sworn agent in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions, is punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 352-5

In all cases, the offender may also be ordered to make reparation, either at the first hearing or in writing, and the term of imprisonment imposed against him is counted only from the day on which the reparation takes place. When the insult is publicly perpetrated, the maximum penalties provided for are imposed.

Article 352-6

The following cannot give rise to prosecutions for insults: parliamentary debates; speeches during electoral campaigns without imputation of fact concerning strictly private life; judicial debates; pronouncements or writings produced before the courts; the faithful and good faith account of these debates and speeches with the exception of defamation proceedings; the publication of judicial decisions including those rendered in defamation matters;
the official report made in good faith by a person duly designated to conduct an inquiry and within the framework of that inquiry; the imputation made in good faith by a superior or his subordinate; information given in good faith about a person or a third party who has a personal or official interest in knowing it or who has the power to remedy an alleged injustice; criticism of a work, a performance, an opinion of any kind publicly expressed provided that such criticism does not constitute a personal attack.

Article 352-7

For the offences provided for in this chapter, the public prosecution is barred after three full months from the commission of the offence or from the day of the last act of prosecution or investigation.

CHAPITRE 3 : DES VIOLENCES ENVERS LES DEPOSITAIRES DE L'AUTORITE PUBLIQUE

Article 353-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, even without weapons and without resulting in injuries, commits violence or assault on a magistrate, an assessor, or any person sitting in a judicial formation in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions.
The maximum penalties are always imposed if the assault or violence takes place at the hearing or within the precincts of a court or tribunal.
The court seized may also impose the prohibition from exercising civil rights, the prohibition from residing as well as from exercising any public employment for a period which may not exceed five years.

Article 353-2

Are punished by a term of imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, violence or assault of the type provided for in Article 353-1 above, directed against a public service agent, if they take place in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions.

Article 353-3

If the violence or assault committed against the officials and agents designated in Articles 353-1 and 353-2 above has caused an incapacity for work equal to twenty-one days or more, the penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years.
If death has resulted, the penalty is imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years.

Article 353-4

In cases where such violence or assault has not caused bloodshed, injuries or illness, the perpetrators are punished by a term of imprisonment from five years to ten years.
If the violence or assault was committed with premeditation or ambush and has caused bloodshed, injuries or illness, the perpetrators are punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty years.

Article 353-5

Is punished by a term of life imprisonment, if the blows were struck or the injuries inflicted on officials or agents designated in Articles 353-1 and 353-2 above in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions with the intention of causing death.

CHAPITRE 4

DE LA DEGRADATION DES MONUMENTS, DES ACTES DE VANDALISME ET DES MANIFESTATIONS ILLICITES

Section 1 : De la dégradation de monuments Article 354-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who voluntarily destroys, mutilates or degrades: either monuments, statues, paintings or other objects intended for public utility or decoration and erected or placed by the public authority or with its authorization; or monuments, statues, paintings or other objects of art of any kind placed in museums, places reserved for worship or other buildings open to the public; or monuments, sites, paintings or other natural objects registered or classified as national heritage.

Section 2 : Des actes de vandalisme Article 354-2

The act of vandalism is the act of damaging, destroying, degrading or voluntarily deteriorating property belonging to another during demonstrations on the public highway.
Vandalism is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 354-3

The offence of act of vandalism applies: to acts of vandalism as provided for in the following article; to the organizers of unlawful demonstrations on the public highway, during which acts of vandalism have been committed;
to the perpetrators and accomplices of acts of vandalism; to persons who enter a demonstration even if lawful, with the intention of committing or having other participants commit acts of vandalism.

Article 354-4

The following acts committed during demonstrations on the public highway constitute acts of vandalism: destruction or damage caused to property, movable or immovable, private or public; destruction of registers, minutes or acts of the public authority.

Section 3 : Des manifestations licites et illicites Article 354-5

A demonstration is lawful when the organizers have made a declaration thereof to the competent administrative authority under the conditions provided for by the laws and regulations in force relating to freedom of assembly and demonstration on the public highway.

Article 354-6

For the purposes of this section, a demonstration is unlawful when: the organizers have not taken the precaution of making a declaration thereof to the competent administrative authority under the conditions provided for by law; the demonstration has been prohibited by the competent administrative authority after the declaration has been made to it; the organizers have made an incomplete or inaccurate declaration of a nature to mislead as to the object or conditions of the planned demonstration.

Article 354-7

The organizers of an unlawful demonstration as defined in Article 354-6 above are liable to penalties of imprisonment from six months to two years

and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.
The penalty is imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs for persons who have voluntarily participated therein.

Article 354-8

Where, as a result of an unlawful demonstration or one prohibited by the administrative police authority, acts of vandalism have been committed, the following are punished by a sentence: of imprisonment from one year to four years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs for the organizers of that demonstration who did not give the order to disperse as soon as they became aware of such violence or assault, destruction or damage; of imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs for persons who continue to participate actively in that demonstration after the commencement of and with knowledge of the violence, assault, destruction or damage.

Article 354-9

Persons found guilty of one of the offences referred to in this chapter are jointly and severally liable for civil reparations.

CHAPITRE 5

BREAKING OF SEALS AND REMOVAL OF ITEMS FROM PUBLIC DEPOSITORIES

Article 355-1

Any person who intentionally breaks or attempts to break seals or is an accomplice thereto is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

If it is the custodian himself who breaks or attempts to break seals or is an accomplice thereto, the sentence of imprisonment is from two years to five years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 355-2

Where seals affixed either by administrative order or pursuant to a court order have been broken, the negligent custodian is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

Article 355-3

Any theft committed by means of breaking seals is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.
Any theft of a seal is punished by the same sentence.

Article 355-4

Any person who removes, destroys or takes away, outside the cases provided for by law, procedural documents or other documents, registers, deeds and effects contained in archives, court registries or public depositories or entrusted to a public depositary in that capacity is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 355-5
