Where the offence provided for in Article 355-4 above has been facilitated by the negligence of court clerks, archivists and other public depositaries, they are punished by a sentence of imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

Article 355-6

If the act is the work of the depositary himself, he is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 355-7

If the breaking of seals, the removal, taking away or destruction of the seal or of the item kept in a public depository has been committed with violence against persons, the sentence is imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs without prejudice to other convictions in respect of the violence.

CHAPITRE 6

OFFENCES RELATING TO BURIALS, SEPULCHRES AND VIOLATION OF RESPECT DUE TO THE DEAD

Article 356-1

Any person who: violates the integrity of a corpse by any means whatsoever; commits a violation or desecration, by any means whatsoever, of tombs, sepulchres, cinerary urns or monuments erected in memory of the dead is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.
Any person who, without a permit issued by the civil registrar, causes a deceased person to be buried is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.
The same sentences are pronounced against those who contravene in any manner whatsoever the legislative and regulatory provisions relating to burials.

Article 356-2

The offences defined in Article 356-1 above are punished by a sentence of imprisonment from three years to ten years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs where they have been committed by reason of the membership or non-membership, true or

supposed, of the deceased persons in a particular ethnic group, nation, race or religion.

Article 356-3

Any person who conceals the corpse of a victim of homicide or who died as a result of assault and battery is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.
Any person who retains without lawful reason in his possession the corpse of a person is also punished by the same sentence.

Article 356-4

Any person who is guilty of mutilation of a corpse is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.
If the mutilations made were for the purpose of trafficking or trade in bones or any other part of the human body, the sentence of imprisonment is from five years to ten years.

CHAPITRE 7: OFFENCES AGAINST THE ENVIRONMENT

Article 357-1

Any person who, through inattention, carelessness or negligence, has harmed the health of humans, animals or plants by altering either the balance of the natural environment or the essential qualities of the soil, water or air is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 357-2

Any person who involuntarily destroys, degrades or damages woods, forests, heathlands, scrublands, plantations, fields or reforestation by the effect of an

explosion, a fire or any other means caused by failure to comply with an obligation of prudence or safety imposed by law or regulation is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.
The same sentences are applicable where the offence described above has resulted in the death of wild animals.
The sentence is imprisonment from one year to six years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs if it concerns fully protected wild animals.
In the case of deliberate violation of an obligation of prudence or safety provided for by law or regulation, the sentences incurred are increased to imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.
In the latter case, the offence is punished by the same sentences where it has resulted in the death of wild animals.
The sentence is imprisonment from three years to seven years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs if it concerns fully protected wild animals.
The attempt is punished by the same sentences.

Article 357-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to seven years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who voluntarily destroys, degrades or deteriorates woods, forests, heaths, scrubland, plantations, fields or reforestation by the effect of an explosion, a fire or any other means. The same penalties are applicable when the offence has resulted in the death of wild animals.
The term of imprisonment is five years to ten years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs, if the offence has caused irreversible damage to the environment.
The same penalties are applicable when the offence has resulted in the death of fully protected wild animals.
The attempt of the offences provided for in this article is punishable.

CHAPITRE 8 : DES AUTRES ATTEINTES A L'ORDRE PUBLIC

Section 1

Des infractions à la réglementation des maisons de jeux et des loteries non autorisées par la loi

Article 358-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, without authorization and in a public place or place open to the public: operates a house of games of chance; operates devices whose operation is essentially based on chance and which are intended to provide a gain for a stake; organizes lotteries, bets or raffles.
In all cases, the funds or effects that are found exposed, the furniture, instruments, devices used and the movable objects with which the premises are furnished or decorated are confiscated for the benefit of the public treasury.
Raffles and games organized for charitable purposes on the occasion of regular declared events are authorized by right.

Article 358-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs anyone who derives his subsistence from practicing or facilitating on the public highway, in a public place or place open to the public, the exercise of illicit games.

Section 2 : Des délits des fournisseurs des forces armées Article 358-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, all without prejudice to heavier penalties in case of intelligence with the enemy, anyone charged, as a member of a company, with supplies,

enterprises or administrations on behalf of the armed forces, who, without having been compelled by force majeure, does not fulfill his obligations.
The same penalties are applicable to agents of suppliers when the cessation of service results from their act.

Article 358-4

When public officials or agents appointed or salaried by the government assist the perpetrators in causing the service to fail, the penalty is increased to the maximum without prejudice to heavier penalties in case of intelligence with the enemy.

Article 358-5

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, even though the service has not failed, anyone who through his negligence, delays deliveries or works or who commits fraud on the nature, quality or quantity of works, labor or things supplied.

Article 358-6

The offences provided for in this section may only be prosecuted upon denunciation by the government.

Section 3

Des infractions relatives à l'industrie, au commerce et aux enchères publiques

Article 358-7

Is punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs and the confiscation of goods, anyone who commits a violation of the regulations relating to products intended for export and which has the purpose of guaranteeing their good quality, their nature, their quantity and their dimensions.

Article 358-8

Is guilty of illicit speculation and punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, directly or through an intermediary, operates or attempts to operate the artificial increase or decrease of prices of foodstuffs, goods or public or private securities by: false or slanderous news or information knowingly spread among the public; offers thrown on the market with the intention of disturbing prices; offers of prices higher than what the sellers are asking; any fraudulent ways or means whatsoever; or by exercising or attempting to exercise either individually or by meeting or coalition, an action on the market with the aim of procuring a gain that would not be the result of the natural play of supply and demand.

Article 358-9

The perpetrator is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs, when the increase or decrease has been operated or attempted on grains, flours, farinaceous substances, foodstuffs, beverages, pharmaceutical products, fuels or commercial fertilizers.
The term of imprisonment is ten years and the fine fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, if the speculation relates to foodstuffs or goods not falling within the usual exercise of the profession of the perpetrator of the offence.

Article 358-10

Is prohibited any advertising made in bad faith containing, in any form whatsoever, allegations, indications or presentations that are false or likely to mislead, when these relate to one or more of the following elements: existence, nature, composition, substantial qualities, content of useful principles, species, origin, quantity, mode and date of manufacture, properties; price and conditions of sale of goods or services that are the subject of the advertising, conditions of their use, results that can be expected

from their use, reasons or procedures of the sale or provision of services; scope of commitments made by the advertiser, identity, quality or aptitudes of the manufacturer, resellers, promoters or service providers.

Article 358-11

The advertiser, on whose behalf the advertising is disseminated, is responsible, on a principal basis, for the offence committed and is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.
These penalties are applicable to advertising agents, owners of advertising media, resellers and service providers.

Article 358-12

The cessation of advertising may be ordered either on requisition of the public prosecutor, or ex officio by the investigating judge or the court seized of the prosecution.

Article 358-13

In the cases provided for by articles 358-9, 358-10, 358-11 and 358-12 above, the court seized may in addition pronounce the prohibition from exercising civil rights and from serving in functions, employment or public offices for a period that may not exceed five years and/or apply the provisions of article 214-17 of this law.

Article 358-14

Is punishable by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who: counterfeits a trademark, service mark or trade mark, or fraudulently affixes a mark belonging to another; uses a mark without authorization from the owner, even with the addition of words such as "formula, style, system, imitation, type".

However, the use of a mark by manufacturers of accessories to indicate the intended purpose of the product is not punishable; holds without legitimate reason products which he knew bore a counterfeited or fraudulently affixed mark, or knowingly sells, offers for sale, supplies or offers to supply products or services under such a mark.

Article 358-15

Is punishable by imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who diverts the clientele of another in commercial or industrial matters: by using inaccurate or misleading titles, distinctive signs, marks or professional names to create belief in particular qualities or capacities; by resorting to measures likely to create confusion with the goods, processes or products, activities or business of another; by disparaging the goods, processes, activities or business of another, or by giving inaccurate or misleading information about his own in order to gain advantage to the detriment of his competitors.

Article 358-16

The maximum penalty is doubled if the diversion of clientele is carried out: by granting or offering to employees, agents or auxiliaries of another advantages which should not be due to them, in order to induce them to discover or reveal a secret of manufacture, organization or operation; by disclosing or exploiting such secrets learned or discovered under the conditions referred to in the preceding point.

Article 358-17

Is punishable by imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who in auctions for the ownership, usufruct or lease of immovable or movable property of an enterprise, operation or service of any kind, obstructs, disturbs, attempts to obstruct or disturb the freedom of bidding or tendering by acts of violence, violence or threats, either before or during the bidding or tendering.
Is punishable by the same penalties anyone who, by gift, promise, collusion or fraudulent maneuver, removes or attempts to remove bidders, limits or attempts to limit bids or tenders, as well as the person who receives such gifts or accepts such promises.

Article 358-18

Is punishable by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine equal to at least four times the value of the goods or property, without such fine being less than ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who imports prohibited, expired or unfit for consumption goods or property.
The same penalty applies to anyone who produces or sells or in any manner whatsoever makes available to the population prohibited or unfit for consumption goods or property.
The same penalty is also applicable to anyone who falsifies the expiration date of such goods or property.

TITRE VI

CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC SECURITY CHAPTER 1: OFFENCES RELATING TO TERRORISM

Section 1: Acts of terrorism Article 361-1

The following offences which, by their nature, are intended to intimidate or terrorize a population or to compel a State or an international organization,

to perform or refrain from performing any act, constitute acts of terrorism: hostage-taking; offences against civil aviation, ships, fixed platforms, and any other means of collective transport; offences against persons enjoying international protection including diplomatic agents; offences involving the use of dangerous materials.

Article 361-2

Also constitute acts of terrorism, the following offences when by their context these acts are intended to intimidate or terrorize a population or to compel a State or an international organization, to perform or refrain from performing any act: intentional attacks on life, intentional attacks on the integrity of the person, kidnapping and unlawful confinement as defined by this code; thefts, extortions, destructions, damage and deterioration, as well as computer-related offences provided for by law; criminal conspiracy; offences relating to weapons and explosive products as defined by law.

Article 361-3

The maximum penalties provided for are imposed for the offences referred to in Article 361-2 above.
The court which imposes a custodial sentence for terrorist acts must accompany it with a safety period at least equal to two-thirds of the sentence imposed.
The safety period determines a maximum non-reducible period of detention.

Section 2

Offences against civil aviation, ships, fixed platforms and any other means of collective transport

Article 361-4

Is punishable by imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years, anyone who, being on board an aircraft in flight, a ship or any other means of collective transport, seizes that aircraft, that ship or that means of transport by violence, threat of violence or exercises control over it.
Is punishable by the same penalty anyone who seizes a fixed platform or exercises control over it by violence or threat of violence.

Article 361-5

Is punishable by imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years, anyone who: destroys an aircraft in service or not at an airport serving civil aviation or causes to such an aircraft damage which renders it incapable of flight or which is likely to endanger its safety in flight; places or causes to be placed, by any means whatsoever, on an aircraft in service or not, parked, a device or substances capable of destroying the said aircraft or causing it damage which renders it incapable of flight or which is likely to endanger its safety in flight; destroys or damages air navigation facilities or services of an airport or disrupts their operation, if any of these acts is likely to endanger the safety of an aircraft in service or of civil aviation; performs an act of violence against a person on board an aircraft, if that act is likely to endanger the safety of the aircraft or of civil aviation.
The same penalties are applicable when the facts set out above concern a fixed platform, a ship or any other means of collective transport.

Article 361-6

If the acts provided for in Articles 361-4 and 361-5 above result in injuries or illnesses, the penalty is imprisonment from twenty-one to thirty years.
If death has resulted, the penalty is life imprisonment.

Article 361-7

An aircraft is considered to be in flight from the moment when, boarding being completed, all its external doors have been closed until the moment when one of these doors is opened for disembarkation. In the case of a forced landing, the flight is deemed to continue until the competent authority takes charge of the aircraft as well as the persons and property on board.
An aircraft is considered to be in service from the moment when ground personnel or the crew begins to prepare it for a specific flight until the expiration of a period of twenty-four hours following any landing. The period of service extends in any event to the entirety of the time during which the aircraft is in flight within the meaning of the preceding paragraph.
The term ship means a vessel of any type whatsoever which is not permanently attached to the sea-bed and includes dynamically supported craft, submersible vehicles and all other floating craft.
The expression fixed platform means an artificial island, an installation or a structure permanently attached to the sea-bed for the purposes of exploration or exploitation of resources or for other economic or scientific purposes.

Article 361-8

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, by communicating information which he knew to be false, compromises the safety of a fixed platform, an aircraft in service, a ship or any other means of collective transport in service.

Section 3

Offences against persons enjoying international protection

Article 361-9

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years whoever:	commits or threatens to commit a kidnapping or any other attack against a person or the liberty of a person enjoying international protection;	commits or threatens to commit, by resorting to violence, against the official premises, private residence or means of transport of a person enjoying international protection, an attack likely to endanger his person or liberty.
Is punished by the same penalty whoever threatens to commit a murder against a person enjoying international protection.
If injuries or illnesses result from the above acts, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one to thirty years.
If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

Article 361-10

The expression "person enjoying international protection" means:	any Head of State, including each member of a collegial body performing, under the constitution of the State concerned, the functions of Head of State;	any head of government or any minister for foreign affairs, when such person is in a foreign State as well as members of his family who accompany him;	any representative, official or official personality of a State and any official, official personality or other agent of an international organization, who at the date and place where an offence is committed against his person, his official premises, his private residence or his means of transport, is entitled under international law to

special protection against any attack on his person, liberty or dignity as well as members of his family who form part of his household.

Section 4: Hostage-taking Article 361-11

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years whoever seizes a person or detains him and threatens to kill him, injure him or continue to detain him in order to compel a third party, namely a State, an international organization, a population, a natural or legal person or a group of persons, to do or abstain from doing any act as an explicit or implicit condition for the release of the hostage.
If injuries or illnesses result from the above acts, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years.
If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

Section 5: Offences by use of dangerous materials Article 361-12

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years whoever:	uses against a ship or a fixed platform or discharges from a ship, explosives, radioactive materials or biological, chemical or nuclear weapons, in a manner likely to cause death or serious bodily or material damage;	uses on board a ship or a fixed platform explosives, radioactive materials or biological, chemical or nuclear weapons, in a manner likely to cause death or serious bodily or material damage;	discharges from a ship or a fixed platform, hydrocarbons, liquefied natural gas or other harmful or potentially dangerous substances, which are not covered by the preceding paragraph, in such quantity or

concentration as is likely to cause death or serious bodily or material damage;	uses a ship in a manner which causes serious material damage;	threatens to commit any of the offences referred to in the preceding bullet points.
If injuries or illnesses have resulted from the above acts, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years.
If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

Article 361-13

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of twenty-one to thirty years whoever transports on board a ship:	explosives or radioactive materials, knowing that they are intended to cause or threaten to cause death, serious bodily or material damage;	any biological, chemical or nuclear weapon knowingly;	source material or special fissionable products, equipment or materials specially designed or prepared for the processing, use or production of special fissionable products, knowing that these materials, products or equipment are intended for a nuclear explosive activity or any other illicit nuclear activity;	equipment, materials or software, or related technologies which contribute significantly to the design, manufacture or delivery of a biological, chemical or nuclear weapon.
If injuries or illnesses have resulted from the above acts, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one to thirty years.
If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

Article 361-14

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years anyone who: holds, transfers, alters, transfers ownership of, disperses, unlawfully uses or threatens to use nuclear or radioactive materials, resulting in or capable of resulting in death or serious injury to others or substantial damage to property or the environment; manufactures or holds a device, with the intention of causing the death of a person or causing them serious bodily harm or with the intention of causing substantial damage to property or the environment; uses radioactive materials or devices in any manner whatsoever, uses or damages a nuclear installation, so as to release or risk releasing radioactive materials, with the intention of causing the death of a person or causing them serious bodily harm or with the intention of causing substantial damage to property or the environment or with the intention of compelling a natural or legal person, an international organization or a government to perform an act or to refrain from doing so; fraudulently removes or threatens to remove, diverts or unlawfully appropriates nuclear materials; unlawfully transports, sends or moves nuclear materials to or from a State; unlawfully commits or threatens to commit an act against a nuclear installation or disrupts its operation, an act by which the perpetrator knows that it may cause death or serious injury to others or substantial damage to property or the environment as a result of exposure to nuclear materials through threat, use of force or any other form of intimidation; demands the surrender of radioactive materials or devices or nuclear installations by resorting to threat in circumstances that make it credible or to the use of force;
introduces into the atmosphere, on the ground, in the subsoil, in food or food components, in water or in objects of use, a substance likely to endanger the health of humans or animals or the natural environment, when this act is intended to intimidate or terrorize a population or to compel a State or an international organization to perform or refrain from performing any act whatsoever.
If injuries or illnesses result from the above acts, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years.
If death has resulted, the penalty is life imprisonment.

Section 6: Preparatory and support acts Article 361-15

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years, anyone who provides or facilitates the provision of weapons, knowing that they may be used for the commission of one of the offences provided for in this chapter.

Article 361-16

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years, anyone who recruits, maintains or provides training to another person to commit or participate in the commission of one of the offences provided for in this chapter or asks them to commit or participate in the commission of one of these offences or to join an association or a group, with the intention or knowing that the purpose of this recruitment or this request is to participate in the commission of one or more offences provided for in this chapter.

Article 361-17

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years:	the national who travels or attempts to travel to a State other than their State of residence or of which they are a national, or any person who leaves or attempts to leave the national territory to travel to a State other than their State of residence or nationality, with the intention of committing,

organizing or preparing acts of terrorism, or in order to participate therein or to provide or receive training in terrorism;	any person who deliberately provides or collects, by any means whatsoever, directly or indirectly, funds that they intend to use or which they know will be used to finance the travel of persons who are traveling to a State other than their State of residence or of which they are nationals, with the intention of committing, organizing or preparing acts of terrorism, or in order to participate therein or to provide or receive training in terrorism;	any person who, on the national territory, deliberately organizes the travel of persons who are traveling to a State other than their State of residence or of which they are nationals, with the intention of committing, organizing or preparing acts of terrorism, or in order to participate therein or to provide or receive training in terrorism, or participation in other activities that facilitate these acts, including recruitment.

Article 361-18

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, the act by any person of being unable to justify resources corresponding to their lifestyle, while being in regular contact with one or more persons engaging in one or more of the terrorist acts defined in this chapter.

Article 361-19

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the act of publicly glorifying terrorist acts.
When the acts have been committed using an online public communication service or through written or audiovisual press, the term of imprisonment is eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 361-20

Constitutes an act of terrorism punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the act of training or being trained in the handling of weapons or in any form of combat, in the manufacture or use of explosive, incendiary, nuclear, radiological, biological or chemical substances or in the piloting of aircraft for terrorist purposes.

Article 361-21

Constitutes an act of terrorism punished by a term of imprisonment of three years to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) francs to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the act of habitually consulting, for terrorist purposes, one or more online public communication services or possessing documents directly inciting the commission of acts of terrorism or glorifying them.

Section 7: Financing of terrorism Article 361-22

Offences relating to the financing of terrorism are governed by the legislative and regulatory provisions relating to the fight against the financing of terrorism in Burkina Faso.

Section 8: Particular provision Article 361-23

For the offences provided for in this chapter, the court seized may, in the event of conviction, order the freezing, confiscation of property and prohibition of residence or territory.

CHAPTER 2: CRIMINAL ASSOCIATION AND ASSISTANCE TO CRIMINALS

Article 362-1

Any association or agreement whatever its duration and the number of its members, formed or established for the purpose of preparing or committing crimes against persons or property, constitutes the crime of criminal association which exists by the mere fact of the resolution to act decided in common.

Article 362-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any individual who is part of the association or agreement defined in Article 362-1 above.
The imprisonment is eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, for the leaders of the association or agreement or for those who have exercised any command therein.

Article 362-3

Benefit from an absolute excuse those perpetrators who, before any attempt to commit a crime that is the object of the association or agreement and before any prosecution, were the first to reveal to the authorities the established agreement or the existence of the association.

Article 362-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowingly harbours a person whom he knows to have committed a crime or an offence and whom he knows to be sought by justice for that reason, or who removes or attempts to remove that person from searches or arrest, or helps him to hide or to flee.
The above provision does not apply to the spouse, relatives or in-laws of the perpetrator of the crime or offence up to and including the fourth degree.

CHAPITRE 3 : DE LA REBELLION

Article 363-1

Any attack or resistance carried out with violence or assault against officials or representatives of public authority acting for the execution of orders or ordinances emanating from that authority or of laws, regulations, judicial decisions or warrants of justice constitutes rebellion.
Threats of violence of a serious nature are assimilated to violence itself.

Article 363-2

Is punished by imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, rebellion committed by one or two persons.
The term of imprisonment is one year to three years and the fine five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, when the rebellion is committed in a gathering of more than two persons.
If the rebellion is committed by more than twenty persons, the perpetrators are punished by a term of imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 363-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any person who commits a rebellion while carrying a weapon.
The term of imprisonment is two years to five years and the fine seven hundred thousand (700,000) to seven million (7,000,000) CFA francs, if in the gathering, more than two individuals are carrying weapons.
If the rebellion is committed by more than twenty persons and there is carrying of weapons, the term of imprisonment is eleven years to twenty-one years and the fine three million (3,000,000) to fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 363-4

For the offences provided for in this chapter, the court seized may pronounce the prohibition from exercising civil rights and from serving in functions, employments or public offices for a duration that may not exceed five years.

Article 363-5

Persons found carrying concealed weapons, having been part of a group or gathering deemed unarmed, are individually punished as in the case of an armed troop or gathering.

Article 363-6

Anyone who incites rebellion either by speeches delivered in public places or meetings, or by placards, posters, leaflets or writings, is punished as an accomplice to rebellion.

Article 363-7

There may be pronounced against the instigators, leaders of the rebellion, a residence ban for a duration that may not exceed five years.

Article 363-8

In the case of rebellion in a group, no penalty is pronounced against rebels who have no role to play in the group, who withdrew at the first warning from the public authority, or who were seized outside the place of rebellion without resistance and without weapons.

Article 363-9

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by assault, opposes the execution of works ordered or authorized by the public authority.
Those who, by gathering, threats or violence, oppose the execution of these works, are punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

CHAPITRE 4

DE L'EVASION ET AUTRES VIOLATIONS DES REGLEMENTS EN MATIERE D'ADMINISTRATION PENITENTIAIRE

Article 364-1

Constitutes an escape the act, by a detainee, of evading the custody to which he is subject.
Escape is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.
When the escape is attempted or carried out by violence, breaking and entering or corruption, even if these offences were committed, in concert with the detainee, by a third party, the imprisonment is three years to five years and the fine five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.
For the application of this article, is considered as detained any person:	who is placed in police custody;	who is in the process of or being presented to the judicial authority at the end of police custody or in execution of a warrant to bring or arrest;	who has been notified of a committal warrant, an arrest warrant or a provisional custody order continuing to produce effect;	who is executing a custodial sentence or who has been arrested to execute that sentence;	who is placed under extradition detention;	who is the subject of a writ of capias;	who is placed under a community service regime.

Article 364-2

The sentence pronounced for escape is cumulative with any temporary custodial sentence imposed for the offence that motivated the arrest or detention.
If the prosecution for this latter offence results in an order or decision of no case to answer, a decision of acquittal or discharge, the duration of the pre-trial detention served on that count is not credited against the duration of the sentence pronounced for escape or attempted escape.

Article 364-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any person serving as escort or manning the posts who, through negligence, permits or facilitates an escape.

Article 364-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three years to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs the act, by any person responsible for his supervision, of facilitating or preparing, even by voluntary abstention, the escape of a detainee.
These provisions are also applicable to any person authorized by his functions to enter a penitentiary establishment or to approach, in any capacity whatsoever, detainees.
The perpetrator is subject to prohibition from any public employment or any public function for a duration that may not exceed five years.

Article 364-5

Is punishable by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs the act of providing a detainee with any means to escape from the custody to which he was subject.

If the assistance thus provided is accompanied by violence, breaking and entering or corruption, the imprisonment is from three to five years and the fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.
If this assistance consists of the provision or use of a weapon or an explosive, incendiary or toxic substance, the imprisonment is from five to ten years and the fine from three million (3,000,000) to ten million five hundred thousand (10,500,000) CFA francs.

Article 364-6

All those who knowingly provide or facilitate an escape are jointly and severally liable for payment of damages owed to the victim or his beneficiaries in compensation for the harm caused by the offense for which the escapee was detained.
In addition, a residence ban may be imposed for a period not exceeding five years.

Article 364-7

Is punishable by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs the act, wherever it occurs, of delivering or causing to reach a detainee, or receiving from him and transmitting sums of money, correspondence, objects or substances of any kind as well as communicating by any means with a detained person, outside cases authorized by regulations.
The penalty is imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs if the perpetrator is responsible for supervising detainees or if he is authorized by his duties to enter a penitentiary establishment or to approach, in any capacity whatsoever, detainees.

Article 364-8

Is punishable by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs the act of entering a penitentiary establishment or climbing over

its enclosure, without being authorized by virtue of legislative or regulatory provisions or having been authorized by the competent authorities.

Article 364-9

The attempt of the offenses provided for in this chapter is punishable.

TITRE VII

OFFENSES AGAINST CURRENCY, MARKS, SECURITIES OR OTHER FIDUCIARY VALUES ISSUED BY PUBLIC AUTHORITY AND FORGERY

CHAPITRE 1: COUNTERFEITING OF CURRENCY

Section 1: General provisions Article 371-1

The provisions of this chapter are intended to punish counterfeiting of currency and other offenses against monetary signs. They apply to offenses committed:	on national territory;	on the territory of other member States of WAEMU;	abroad, outside member States of WAEMU, according to the distinctions and conditions provided by the legislation in force.

Article 371-2

For the purposes of this chapter, the following are understood as:	competent authorities: bodies which, by virtue of a legal or regulatory provision, are authorized to carry out or order the acts or measures provided for by the uniform law relating to counterfeiting of currency;	BCEAO or Central Bank: the Central Bank of West African States;	counterfeiting: the manufacture of a monetary sign imitating a monetary sign issued by the BCEAO or any other authorized foreign issuing institute;
foreigner: any person who lives in the Union without having the nationality of one of the member States of WAEMU;	falsification: the alteration of a monetary sign with a view to modifying its substance or weight;	counterfeit currency (counterfeit banknotes or counterfeit coins): banknotes and coins which have the appearance of banknotes or coins issued by the BCEAO or any other authorized foreign issuing body or, although intended to be put into circulation, have not yet been issued by the institutions authorized for this purpose;	counterfeiting of currency:
•	all fraudulent acts (counterfeiting and falsification) of manufacture or alteration of monetary signs issued by the BCEAO or any other foreign issuing institute authorized for this purpose, whatever the means employed to produce the result;
•	the putting into circulation of counterfeit currency with full knowledge;
•	the act of holding, importing, exporting, transporting, receiving or obtaining counterfeit currency, with the aim of putting it into circulation with full knowledge;
•	the fraudulent act of manufacturing, holding, receiving or obtaining instruments, objects, computer programs or any other process intended, by their nature, for the manufacture of counterfeit currency, the alteration of currencies or the manufacture of security elements of monetary signs;	FCFA: Franc of the African Financial Community;	putting counterfeit currency into circulation: the issuance of counterfeit currency, regardless of the number of banknotes or coins circulated;	reproduction of monetary signs: creation of any tangible or intangible image which presents a resemblance to a banknote or the image of a coin, whatever the size of the image, the materials, instruments and techniques used to produce it and

regardless of whether the patterns, letters and symbols appearing on the monetary sign have been modified or not;	monetary signs: banknotes or coins having or having had legal tender;	WAMU or Union: West African Monetary Union.

Section 2: Incriminations and applicable penalties Article 371-3

The counterfeiting or falsification of banknotes or coins having legal tender on the national territory of a member State of WAEMU or abroad is punishable by fixed-term imprisonment from ten years to twenty years and a fine of an amount equal to ten times the value of said signs without being less than twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.
Is punishable by the same penalties, the counterfeiting or falsification of banknotes or coins which, although intended to be put into circulation, have not yet been issued by the institutions authorized for this purpose.
If the offender benefits from mitigating circumstances, the penalty cannot be less than five years' imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.
Suspended sentence cannot be granted.

Article 371-4

The custodial sentence provided for in the first two paragraphs of the preceding article is accompanied by a safety period of seven years.
During the safety period, the convicted person cannot benefit from provisions relating to the method of sentence adjustment, in particular those concerning the suspension or splitting of the sentence, external placement, release permits, semi-liberty and conditional release.

Article 371-5

The counterfeiting or falsification of banknotes or coins having had legal tender on national territory or abroad, is punishable by imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine of an amount equal to ten times the value of said signs, without being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 371-6

The manufacture of banknotes and coins carried out using authorized installations or equipment intended for this purpose, when it is carried out in violation of the conditions set by the institutions authorized to issue these monetary signs and without their agreement, is punishable by the penalties provided for in Article 371-3 above.

Article 371-7

The putting into circulation, use, display, distribution, importation, exportation, transport, receipt, possession, with full knowledge, of counterfeit or falsified monetary signs having legal tender, is punished by imprisonment of five years to seven years and a fine of an amount equal to ten times the value of said signs without being less than five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.
When they are committed by an organized gang, the offences provided for in the preceding paragraph are punished by the penalties provided for in Article 371-3 above.
The putting into circulation, use, display, distribution, importation, exportation, transport, receipt, possession, of counterfeit or falsified monetary signs having had legal tender, with full knowledge, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of an amount equal to ten times the value of said signs, without being less than one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.
When they are committed by an organized gang, the offences provided for in paragraph 3 of this article are punished by the penalties provided for in Article 371-5 above.

Article 371-8

Are punished by a fine equal to ten times their value, without the amount of the fine being less than two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) CFA francs, those who, having received monetary signs believing them to be good and who, after having learned of their defects, knowingly keep them and refrain from handing them over to the BCEAO or to the competent authorities.
Are punished by a fine equal to ten times their value, without the amount being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, credit institutions, decentralized financial systems, manual exchange agents and postal financial services which, having received during operations with their customers, counterfeit or falsified monetary signs, have not retained them, against receipt, for the purpose of handing them over to the BCEAO or to the competent authorities.

Article 371-9

The putting back into circulation, after having discovered their defects, of counterfeit or falsified banknotes which were believed to be good at the time of receipt, is punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of an amount equal to ten times the value of said signs without being less than five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

Article 371-10

The manufacture, offer, receipt, importation, exportation, or possession, without having been authorized, of marks, materials, apparatus, instruments, computer programs, or any other element specially intended for the manufacture or protection against counterfeiting or falsification of banknotes or coins is punished by imprisonment of five years to seven years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) CFA francs to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 371-11

The manufacture, possession, putting into circulation, use, display, distribution, importation or exportation of unauthorized monetary signs, intended to replace coins or banknotes having legal tender on the national territory or abroad, is punished by imprisonment of five years to seven years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) CFA francs to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.
Is punished by the same penalties, the possession, putting into circulation, use, display, distribution, importation or exportation of banknotes and coins which, although intended to be put into circulation, have not yet been issued by the institutions authorized for this purpose and do not yet have legal tender on the national territory or abroad.

Article 371-12

The manufacture, possession, putting into circulation, use, display, distribution, importation or exportation of printed matter, forms or tokens intended to be accepted as a means of payment, is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) CFA francs to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 371-13

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) CFA francs to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, whoever:	reproduces, totally or partially, by any process whatsoever, monetary signs having legal tender on the national territory or abroad, without the prior authorization of the BCEAO or, in the case of foreign monetary signs, of the authority which issued them;	displays, distributes, imports or exports reproductions of monetary signs, including by way of newspapers, books or prospectuses without the prior authorization of the Central Bank or, in the case of foreign monetary signs, of the authority which issued them;	uses banknotes having legal tender on the national territory or abroad, as a medium for any advertising whatsoever.

Article 371-14

The deliberate deterioration, soiling or overloading of a monetary sign is punished by imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, when it has the effect of rendering it unfit for use as a means of payment.

Article 371-15

The refusal to receive currency having legal tender in a Member State of the Union according to the value for which it has tender is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

Article 371-16

The collection of a commission in consideration for the handing over of monetary signs issued by the BCEAO in exchange for other monetary signs of its issue, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) CFA francs to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 371-17

Are confiscated, whatever the classification of the offence, the counterfeit or falsified monetary signs and others referred to in Articles 371-3 to 371-14 above as well as the metals, papers and other materials found in the possession of the offenders and intended for the commission of similar offences.
Are also confiscated, the instruments having served to commit the offence, except when they have been used without the knowledge of their owner.

Article 371-18

The competent court shall mandatorily pronounce against natural persons found guilty of the offences provided for in Articles 371-3 to 371-12 above, the following additional penalties:	the prohibition from exercising an activity in the banking and financial sector for a period not exceeding twenty years;	the prohibition of residence, permanently, or for a period not exceeding twenty years for foreigners.

It may, in addition, pronounce, against them, the prohibition of civil rights for a period not exceeding twenty years.

Article 371-19

Legal persons other than the State are criminally liable for the offences defined in this, when they are committed on their behalf by their organs or representatives.
The legal person found criminally liable is, without prejudice to the penalties incurred by the natural persons co-perpetrators or accomplices of the same acts, punished by a fine equal to five times the amount provided for natural persons.
The competent court shall pronounce in addition the following additional penalties:	the dissolution of the legal person, when it has been created or diverted from its corporate purpose to commit the offences referred to in Articles 371-3 to 371-12 above;	the permanent closure of the enterprise or for a period between one year and five years.

Article 371-20

The attempt of the offences referred to in this chapter is punishable.

Article 371-21

In case of recidivism, the penalties provided for in this chapter are doubled.
Convictions pronounced by the criminal courts of a Member State of WAEMU for the offences provided for by this law are taken into account for the purposes of recidivism in all other Member States.

Article 371-22

Any person who, having taken part in the offences provided for in Articles 371-3 to 371-12 above, has informed the competent authorities thereof or has revealed the perpetrators before any prosecution, is exempted from penalties. He or she may, nevertheless, be prohibited from residing if he or she has the status of a foreigner.
Any person who, having taken part in the offences referred to in the preceding paragraph, has, after the initiation of prosecution, enabled the arrest of the other participants, may be exempted from penalties, totally or partially. He or she may, nevertheless, be prohibited from residing if he or she has the status of a foreigner.

Article 371-23

When it pronounces a conviction in application of the provisions of this chapter, the competent court may order the posting or dissemination of all or part of the decision or of a press release informing the public of the reasons for and the operative part thereof.
It shall determine, where appropriate, the extracts from the decision and the terms of the press release which must be posted or disseminated.
The posting or dissemination of the decision or press release may include the identity of the victim only with his or her consent or that of his or her legal representative or successors in title.
The posting shall be carried out in the places and for the duration indicated by the court. Unless otherwise decided by the court, the posting may not exceed two months. In case of removal, concealment, or tearing of the posted notices, posting shall be carried out again at the expense of the person found guilty of these acts.
The dissemination of the decision shall be made by one or more press publications or by one or more electronic communication services to the public. The publications or electronic communication services to the public responsible for this dissemination are designated by the court. They may not oppose this dissemination.
The posting and dissemination may be ordered cumulatively.

The posting of the decision pronounced or the dissemination thereof shall be at the expense of the convicted person. The posting or dissemination costs recovered from the latter may not, however, exceed the maximum fine incurred.

Section 3: Applicable procedure Article 371-24

By way of derogation from the provisions of the Code of Criminal Procedure, the correctional courts have jurisdiction to hear the crimes provided for in this chapter.
The procedure followed is that applicable in correctional matters.

Article 371-25

By way of derogation from the provisions of the Code of Criminal Procedure, for the offences provided for in this chapter, the public prosecution is subject to limitation according to the following distinctions:	twenty years for crimes;	ten years for offences.

Article 371-26

When they are seized of cases relating to counterfeiting or discover, during their investigations, counterfeit or falsified monetary signs, the competent authorities are required to transmit to the Central Bank, for analysis and identification, at least one specimen of each type of banknotes or coins suspected to be false.
The provisions of the preceding paragraph are not applicable when there exists only one specimen of a type of banknotes or coins suspected to be false, as long as it is necessary for the establishment of the truth.

Article 371-27

Counterfeit or falsified monetary signs as well as materials and instruments intended to be used for their manufacture, confiscated in application of Article 371-17, are handed over to the Central Bank for their possible destruction, subject to the needs of the administration of justice.

Article 371-28

When the Central Bank recognizes as counterfeit or falsified, monetary signs which are handed over to it, it is authorized to retain them and possibly to destroy them, subject to the needs of the administration of justice.

CHAPTER 2

COUNTERFEITING OF STATE SEALS, PUBLIC OR PRIVATE INSTRUMENTS, HALLMARKS, STAMPS AND MARKS

Article 372-1

Any person who counterfeits a seal of the State or makes use of a counterfeit seal is punished by a term of imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 372-2

Any person who counterfeits or falsifies either one or more national stamps, or one or more hallmarks of the State used to mark gold or silver materials or who makes use of falsified or counterfeit stamps or hallmarks is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 372-3

Any person who, having unlawfully obtained genuine stamps or hallmarks of the State referred to in the preceding article, makes an application or use thereof prejudicial to the rights and interests of the State, its subdivisions and third parties is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 372-4

Any person who is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs:	manufactures the seals, stamps, marks, cachets of the State or of any authority without the written order of the accredited representatives of the State or of that authority;	manufactures, holds, distributes, purchases or sells stamps, seals, marks or cachets likely to be confused with those of the State or of any authority, even foreign.

Article 372-5

Any person who manufactures, sells, hawks, distributes or uses printed matter which, by its format, colour, text, typographical layout or any other characteristic, presents with letterheads or official printed matter in use in institutions, public administrations and the various courts, a resemblance likely to cause confusion in the mind of the public is punished by a term of imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.

Article 372-6

Any person who is punished by a term of imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs:	counterfeits the marks intended to be affixed in the name of the government or of a public service on the various kinds of foodstuffs or goods or who makes use of these false marks;	counterfeits the seal, stamp or mark of any authority or makes use of the counterfeit seal, stamp or mark;	counterfeits or falsifies postage stamps, franking impressions or reply coupons issued by the Burkinabe postal administration, tax stamps

mobile stamped papers	or forms stamps,	impressions, reply coupons, stamped papers or forms counterfeit or falsified.
In addition, the prohibition from exercising civil and professional rights and/or the prohibition from residing for a period which may not exceed five years may be pronounced.

Article 372-7

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, having unduly obtained genuine seals, marks or printed matter provided for in the preceding article, makes or attempts to make fraudulent application or use thereof.
In addition, disqualification from exercising civil and/or professional rights and/or prohibition from residence may be pronounced for a period that may not exceed five years.

Article 372-8

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who: knowingly makes use of postage stamps, revenue stamps or stamped papers or forms that have already been used or who, by any means, alters stamps for the purpose of removing them from cancellation and thus permitting their subsequent reuse; overprints by printing, perforation or any other means Burkinabé postage stamps or other postal fiduciary values, expired or not, or who sells, hawks, offers, distributes, exports postage stamps thus overprinted; counterfeits, imitates or alters stamps, labels, franking impressions or reply coupons issued by the postal service of a foreign country, sells, hawks or distributes said stamps, labels, franking impressions or reply coupons or knowingly makes use thereof.

Article 372-9

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, anyone who is guilty of manufacturing, introducing into Burkina Faso, selling or distributing any objects, tokens, printed matter or forms, obtained by any process whatsoever and which, by their appearance, would present with annuity certificates, shares, bonds, interest shares, dividend or interest coupons relating thereto and generally with fiduciary securities issued by companies or private enterprises, a resemblance of a nature to facilitate the acceptance of said objects, tokens, printed matter or forms in place of the imitated securities.
In addition, disqualification from exercising civil and professional rights and prohibition from residence may be pronounced for a period that may not exceed five years.

Article 372-10

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of twenty-one to thirty years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who counterfeits, falsifies or alters certificates, bonds or obligations issued by the public treasury with its stamp or mark, or interest coupons relating to these certificates, bonds or obligations.

Article 372-11

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of twenty-one to thirty years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, in any manner whatsoever, knowingly participates in the issue, distribution, sale or introduction into Burkina Faso of the certificates, bonds or obligations designated in the preceding article.

Article 372-12

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who manufactures, acquires, possesses or transfers knowingly products or equipment intended for the commission of the offences punished above unless the act constitutes a more serious offence.

Article 372-13

Benefits from an absolute excuse one of the perpetrators of the crimes mentioned in articles 372-10 and 372-11 above who, before the consummation of these crimes and before any prosecution, gives knowledge thereof to the authorities and reveals the identity of the perpetrators or who, even after prosecution has commenced, facilitates the arrest of the other perpetrators.

Article 372-14

In the case of the offences referred to in articles 372-9, 372-10 and 372-12 above, the trial court pronounces the confiscation of products and equipment.
It may in addition order the closure of the establishment for a period that may not exceed five years.

CHAPITRE 3 : DU FAUX

Article 373-1

Constitutes a forgery, any fraudulent alteration of the truth of a nature to cause prejudice and accomplished by any means whatsoever, in a writing or any other medium for the expression of thought which has as its object or which may have the effect of establishing proof of a right or of a fact having legal consequences.

Article 373-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to nine hundred thousand (900,000) CFA francs, anyone who has unduly obtained or attempts to unduly obtain an authentic or public document: either by making false declarations; or by taking a false name or a false qualification; or by providing false information, certificates or attestations.

Article 373-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who has taken the name of a third party in circumstances which have determined or could have determined the recording of a conviction on the criminal record of the latter.
Is punished by the penalties provided for in the first paragraph anyone who, by false declarations relating to the civil status of a person under examination, has knowingly been the cause of the recording of a conviction on the criminal record of another than this person under examination.

Article 373-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs anyone who, by taking a false name or a false qualification, has obtained an extract from the criminal record of a third party.
Is punished by the same penalties anyone who provides imaginary identity information which causes or could have caused erroneous entries on the criminal record.

Article 373-5

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, anyone not a party to the act who makes before a public authority a declaration that he or she knew was not in conformity with the truth.
However, benefits from an absolute excuse anyone who, having made as a witness before a public authority a declaration not in conformity with the truth, retracts before prejudice to another results from the use of the act and before he or she is himself or herself the subject of prosecution.

Article 373-6

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever commits or attempts to commit a forgery in private, commercial or banking writing, under one of the following conditions:
either by counterfeiting or alteration of writing or signature;	either by fabrication of agreements, provisions, obligations or discharges or by their subsequent insertion in these acts;	either by addition, omission or alteration of clauses, declarations or facts that these acts were intended to receive or record;	either by impersonation or substitution of persons.
The penalty is imprisonment of three years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, when the perpetrator is a banker, a director or manager of a company and in general a person who has made a public appeal for the issuance of shares, bonds, vouchers, units or any securities whatsoever, either of a company or of a commercial or industrial enterprise.
There may in addition be pronounced against the perpetrators of the above-mentioned acts the prohibition from exercising civil and professional rights and the prohibition of residence for a period which may not exceed five years.

Article 373-7

In cases of forgery in private, commercial or banking writing, whoever makes use of the document which he knew to be false is punished by the penalties provided for in Article 373-4 above.

Article 373-8

Public writing is the work, or is deemed to be the work of a public agent.
Public agent means any civil or military person who holds a legislative, executive, administrative or judicial mandate, whether appointed or elected, on a permanent or temporary basis, whether remunerated or not, and regardless of their hierarchical level or seniority.
Authentic writing is the work which emanates or is deemed to emanate from a public officer or a person appointed by law to draw up certain acts or make certain findings.
Commercial or banking writing is writing which has the purpose of recording a transaction constituting an act of commerce.

Private writing is that which is neither public, nor authentic, nor commercial.

Article 373-9

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any civil servant or public officer who, in the exercise of his functions, commits a forgery in authentic or public writing:	either by false signature;	either by alteration of acts, writings or signatures;	either by impersonation or substitution of persons;	either by writings made or inserted in registers or in other public acts, after their completion or closure.

Article 373-10

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any civil servant or public officer who, in drafting acts of his function, fraudulently misrepresents the substance or circumstances thereof either:	by writing agreements other than those which were drawn up or dictated by the parties;	by recording as true facts which he knew to be false;	by attesting as having been acknowledged or having occurred in his presence facts which were not;	by voluntarily omitting or modifying declarations received by him.

Article 373-11

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, whoever other than the person designated in the preceding article commits a forgery in authentic or public writing.

Article 373-12

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, in the cases referred to above, makes use of the document which he knew to be false.

Article 373-13

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, lodging house keepers, hoteliers and innkeepers who knowingly register in their registers under false or assumed names the persons lodged with them or who, in collusion with them, omit to register them.
They are in addition civilly liable for restitution, compensation and costs awarded to the victims of crimes and offences committed during their stay by these persons.
The closure of the establishment may be pronounced.

Article 373-14

There may in addition be pronounced against the perpetrators of the above-mentioned acts the prohibition from exercising civil and professional rights and the prohibition of residence for a period which may not exceed five years.

CHAPITRE 4 : DU FAUX TEMOIGNAGE, DU FAUX SERMENT ET DE L'OMISSION DE TEMOIGNER

Article 374-1

False testimony is the voluntary alteration of the truth made under oath by a witness in a deposition that has become irrevocable with the aim of deceiving justice in favour of or to the detriment of one of the parties.

Article 374-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever is guilty of false testimony in criminal matters against the accused or in his favour.

The penalty is imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs if the false witness has received money, any reward or promises.
In case of conviction of the accused to life imprisonment, the false witness who testified against him incurs imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 374-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, whoever is guilty of false testimony in offence matters against the defendant or in his favour.
The maximum penalty is pronounced if the false witness has received money, any reward or promises.

Article 374-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever is guilty of false testimony in simple police matters against the defendant or in his favour.
The maximum penalty is pronounced if the false witness has received money, any reward or promises.

Article 374-5

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, whoever is guilty of false testimony in civil, commercial, social or administrative matters.
The maximum penalty is pronounced if the false witness has received money, any reward or promises.

Article 374-6

Commits the offence of subornation of witness and is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, whoever in any matter, at any stage of proceedings or with a view to a claim or defence in court, uses promises, offers or gifts, pressure, threats, acts of violence, manoeuvres or artifices to induce another to make or deliver a false testimony, declaration or attestation, whether or not the subornation has produced effect, unless the act constitutes complicity in one of the more serious offences provided for in Articles 374-2, 374-3, 374-4 and 374-5 above.

Article 374-7

Is punished by the penalties for false testimony according to the distinctions provided in articles 374-2, 374-3, 374-4 and 374-5 above, the interpreter who, during a trial, knowingly distorts the substance of oral statements or documents translated orally.
Where the distortion is made in the written translation of a document intended or capable of establishing proof of a right or of a fact having legal effects, the interpreter is punished by the penalties for forgery in writing according to the distinctions provided in articles 373-7, 373-8, 373-9 and 373-10 above.

Article 374-8

Is liable to the penalties for false testimony according to the distinctions provided in articles 374-2 to 374-5 above, the expert who, appointed by the judicial authority, gives orally or in writing, at any stage of the proceedings, a false opinion or affirms facts which he knows do not conform to reality.

Article 374-9

The subornation of an interpreter or expert is punished as the subornation of a witness according to the provisions of article 374-6 above.

Article 374-10

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any person to whom an oath is deferred or referred in civil matters and who takes a false oath.

Article 374-11

Any person who, knowing the proof of the innocence of a person provisionally imprisoned or convicted for an offence or crime, voluntarily refrains from immediately bringing testimony thereof to the judicial or police authorities, is punished:	if it concerns a crime, by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs;	if it concerns an offence, by a term of imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.
However, no penalty is incurred by a person who brings his testimony late but spontaneously.
The provisions of this article do not apply:	to relatives in direct line up to and including the fourth degree and their spouses;	to brothers and sisters and their spouses.

CHAPITRE 5 : DE L'USURPATION OU DE L'USAGE IRREGULIER DE FONCTIONS

Article 375-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, unless the act constitutes a more serious offence, any person who, without title,

interferes in public, civil or military functions or performs an act of those functions.

Article 375-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, unless more severe penalties are provided by a special text, any person who, without fulfilling the conditions required to hold it, makes use of or claims a title attached to a legally regulated profession, an official diploma or a quality whose conditions of attribution are fixed by the public authority.

Article 375-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, any person who, without right, publicly wears a regulatory uniform, a distinctive costume of a function or quality, an official insignia or a decoration of a national or foreign order, unless the act is retained as an aggravating circumstance of a more serious offence.

Article 375-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any person who, either in an official act or habitually, unduly attributes to himself a title or an honorary distinction.

Article 375-5
