Theft is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs: when it is facilitated by the condition of a person whose particular vulnerability, due to his age, an illness, an infirmity, a physical or mental deficiency, is apparent or known to its perpetrator; when it is facilitated by the condition of a person whose particular vulnerability, due to a state of pregnancy, is apparent or known to its perpetrator.

Article 611-15

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who counterfeits or alters keys.
The term of imprisonment is four years to ten years and the fine one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, if the offender is a locksmith by profession.
The court seized orders the confiscation of the false keys and the equipment.
The court may further order on a provisional basis for a period not exceeding five years or permanently, the closure of the establishment in the cases referred to in paragraph 2.

Article 611-16

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who steals from fields animals, beasts of burden or draft, large and small livestock or agricultural instruments.

The same penalties are applicable to the theft of wood in cuttings, stones in quarries, as well as to the theft of fish in ponds, fish farms or reservoirs.

Article 611-17

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who steals from fields crops or other useful products of the land whether detached from the soil or not, or from granaries.
If the theft was committed at night, either by several persons, or with the aid of a vehicle or pack animals, the imprisonment is from five years to ten years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 611-18

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who by any means whatsoever fraudulently extracts from a distribution network running water, fuel, electrical energy or any other energy having economic value or fraudulently uses any product emanating from an apparatus for the purpose of transmitting or receiving information.

Article 611-19

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who uses a motorized vehicle without the knowledge or against the will of the owner.
Prosecution takes place only upon complaint by the injured person; withdrawal of the complaint terminates the prosecution.

Article 611-20

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the co-heir or claimant to an estate who fraudulently disposes of all or part of the inheritance before partition.

Article 611-21

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the person under seizure who voluntarily destroys or misappropriates seized objects entrusted to his custody or to that of a third party.

Article 611-22

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any debtor, borrower or third party pledgor who misappropriates or voluntarily destroys an object given as pledge of which he is the owner.

Article 611-23

Is punishable by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, anyone who, having fortuitously found a movable thing, appropriates it without notifying the competent local authority or the owner.
Is punishable by the same penalty anyone who fraudulently appropriates a movable thing that came into his possession by error or fortuitously.

Article 611-24

Is punishable by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who having found a treasure, even on his property, refrains from notifying the public authority within fifteen days of the discovery.
Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any finder who, having or not having notified the public authority, appropriates the treasure, in whole or in part, without having been sent into possession by the competent magistrate.

Article 611-25

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowing that he is in absolute impossibility of paying or who is determined not to pay, has beverages or food served to him which he consumes in whole

or in part in establishments intended for that purpose, even if he is lodged in said establishments.
The same penalty is applicable to one who, knowing that he is in absolute impossibility of paying or who is determined not to pay, has one or more rooms assigned to him in a hotel, inn or camp and effectively occupies them.
However, in the cases provided for in the preceding paragraphs, the occupation of lodging must not exceed fifteen hotel days.

Article 611-26

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) francs to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowing that he is in impossibility of paying or who is determined not to pay, hires a taxi or takes a seat in a public transport vehicle.

Article 611-27

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) francs to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowing that he is in absolute impossibility of paying or who is determined not to pay, has fuels or lubricants served to him by distribution professionals.

Article 611-28

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by force, violence or constraint, extorts the signature or delivery of a writing, an act, computer data, a title or any document whatsoever containing or effecting obligations, dispositions or releases.

Article 611-29

Is guilty of blackmail and punishable by a term of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by means of written or verbal threat, of revelation or defamatory imputation, obtains either the delivery of funds or securities, or the delivery of computer data, or the signature or delivery of the writings provided for in article 611-28 above.

Article 611-30

In all cases provided for in this chapter, the court seized may in addition pronounce the prohibition from exercising civil rights and/or the prohibition of residence for a duration which may not exceed five years.

The attempt of the offenses provided for in this chapter is punishable.

CHAPTER 2: AGGRAVATED BANDITRY

Article 612-1

For the purposes of this chapter, acts of aggravated banditry mean thefts characterized by the use of any weapons whatsoever, any forms of violence against persons or any other mechanical or chemical processes.

Article 612-2

Are notably constitutive of acts of aggravated banditry: theft, when it was committed on public roads or in private vehicles or vehicles used for transporting passengers, correspondence or luggage, in the circumstances provided for in article 612-1 above; theft, when it was preceded, accompanied or followed by another crime; theft committed with use of a weapon; theft committed with carrying of a visible or concealed weapon; theft committed with violence or accompanied by any act of barbarity or torture; theft committed by using mechanical or chemical processes.

Article 612-3

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to life imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, any person found guilty of an act of aggravated banditry as defined in articles 612-1 and 612-2 above.

The provisions of this chapter are not applicable in case of harm to life.

Article 612-4

In case of conviction, the court may pronounce a sentence with partial suspension.
The court that pronounces a firm term of imprisonment must accompany it with a safety period at least equal to half of the sentence pronounced.
The safety period determines a maximum incompressible detention period.

Article 612-5

In matters of aggravated banditry, special protection may be granted to witnesses as well as to their families.

Article 612-6

The offences provided for in this chapter are established and prosecuted in accordance with the provisions of criminal procedure applicable to serious organised crime.

CHAPTER 3: FRAUD AND BREACH OF TRUST

Section 1: Fraud Article 613-1

Any person is guilty of fraud who, either by using a false name or false capacity, or by abusing a true capacity, or by employing fraudulent schemes to deceive a natural or legal person and thereby induce them, to their prejudice or to the prejudice of another, to hand over funds, securities, computer data or any property whatsoever, to provide a service or to consent to an act creating an obligation or discharge.

Article 613-2

Fraud is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty is five years to ten years' imprisonment and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs when the fraud is committed: by a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions or mission; by a person who unduly assumes the capacity of a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission; by a person who has made an appeal to the public with a view to the issue of shares, bonds, debentures, units or any securities whatsoever, either of a company or of a commercial or industrial enterprise; by a person who makes an appeal to the public with a view to the issue of securities or with a view to the collection of funds for humanitarian or social mutual aid purposes; by a person using a means of electronic communication; to the prejudice of a person whose particular vulnerability, due to their age, illness, infirmity, physical or mental deficiency or state of pregnancy, is apparent or known to its perpetrator.
The term of imprisonment is seven years to ten years and the fine five million (5,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs when the fraud is committed by an organised gang.
In all cases, the court seized may order the deprivation of civil rights and/or prohibition from residing in certain areas, for a period which may not exceed five years.
The attempt of the offences provided for in this section is punishable.

Section 2: Breach of trust

Article 613-3

Any person who misappropriates or dissipates to the prejudice of another, funds, securities, computer data or any property whatsoever which have been handed over to them and which they have accepted on condition of returning them, producing them or making a specified use of them, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 613-4

The term of imprisonment is five years to ten years and the fine three million (3,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs when the breach of trust is committed: by a public ministerial officer, a judicial representative, a sequestrator, a business agent, a commercial agent or any person whose profession is to manage the affairs of others, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions or profession; by a person who makes an appeal to the public in order to obtain the handing over of funds or securities either on their own account or as a director (director, administrator, manager in particular) or agent de jure or de facto of an industrial or commercial enterprise; by any other person who, habitually, engages in or lends their assistance, even in an ancillary capacity, to transactions relating to the property of third parties on whose behalf they collect funds or securities; by a person using a means of electronic communication; to the prejudice of an association which makes an appeal to the public with a view to the collection of funds for humanitarian or social mutual aid purposes; to the prejudice of a person whose particular vulnerability, due to their age, illness, infirmity, physical or mental deficiency or state of pregnancy, apparent or known to its perpetrator.

Article 613-5

Any person who, after having produced in a judicial dispute any title, document or memorandum, removes it in any manner whatsoever, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to three months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

Article 613-6

Any person who abuses the needs, weaknesses or passions of a minor or an incapacitated adult, in order to make them subscribe, to their prejudice, obligations, receipts or discharges, for a loan of money or movable things or bills of exchange or any other obligatory instruments, in whatever form this transaction has been made or disguised, is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.
The fine may however be increased to one quarter of the value of the restitutions and damages if this quarter exceeds the maximum provided for in the preceding paragraph.
The court seized may in addition order the deprivation of civil rights, and/or of public functions or employment as well as prohibition from residing in certain areas for a period which may not exceed five years.

Article 613-7

Any person who, abusing a signed blank document which has been entrusted to them, fraudulently writes above it an obligation or discharge or any other act capable of compromising the person or fortune of the signatory, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.

In the case where the signed blank document has not been entrusted to them, the perpetrator is prosecuted as a forger.

CHAPTER 4: RECEIVING STOLEN GOODS AND COUNTERFEITING

Section 1: Receiving stolen goods Article 614-1

Receiving stolen goods is the act of concealing, holding or transmitting a thing or computer data, or of acting as an intermediary in order to transmit them, knowing that this thing or this computer data comes from a crime or an offence.
Also constitutes receiving stolen goods the act, knowingly, of benefiting, by any means, from the proceeds of a crime or an offence.
Receiving stolen goods is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.
The fine may be increased beyond three million (3,000,000) CFA francs without exceeding half the value of the objects received, all without prejudice to heavier penalties if applicable in case of complicity in a crime.

Article 614-2

Heirs who divert or conceal the effects of an estate or falsely evaluate the composition of an estate, with the aim of harming the interests of other co-heirs, are punished by the penalties provided for in Article 614-1 above.
The accomplices of the perpetrators of the offences provided for in the preceding paragraph, when they exercise prerogatives of parental authority, guardianship or curatorship are punished by the maximum penalties provided for.
The penalties of paragraph 2 are applied to the liquidator of an estate.

Article 614-3

In the case where a criminal penalty is applicable to the act which procured the things received, the receiver is punished by the penalty attached by law to the crime and to the circumstances of the crime of which they had knowledge at the time of receiving.

Section 2: Counterfeiting

Article 614-4

Constitutes the offence of counterfeiting and is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any edition of writing, musical composition, drawing, painting or any other production published in Burkina Faso or abroad, printed or engraved in whole or in part, made in bad faith and in disregard of the laws and regulations relating to artistic or literary property.
The exportation or importation of counterfeit works is punished by the same penalty.
Also punished by the same penalty is any reproduction, representation, dissemination, translation, adaptation by any means whatsoever of a work of the mind in violation of copyright as defined and regulated by law.

Article 614-5

The penalty is imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to four million (4,000,000) CFA francs, if it is established that the perpetrator habitually engages in the acts referred to in the preceding article.

Article 614-6

Counterfeit works as well as receipts or shares of receipts that have given rise to confiscation are handed over to the author or his successors in title to compensate them to that extent for the damage they have suffered; the remainder of their compensation or the full compensation, if there has been no confiscation of material, counterfeit objects or receipts, is settled by ordinary means.
The court seized may order at the request of the civil party the publication of the conviction.

Article 614-7

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone

who discloses without the authorization of the person to whom it belongs, an industrial or commercial fact or process of which he became aware by reason of his employment.

CHAPTER 5: USURY

Article 615-1

Constitutes a usurious loan, any loan or any agreement concealing a loan of money granted, in any matter, at an effective overall interest rate exceeding, on the date of its stipulation, the usury rate.
The usury rate is determined by the Council of Ministers of the West African Monetary Union. It is published in the Official Journal or in a legal notices newspaper, at the initiative of the minister responsible for finance.

Article 615-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these penalties, anyone who grants to another a usurious loan or knowingly provides, in any capacity and in any manner whatsoever, directly or indirectly, his assistance in obtaining or granting a usurious loan.
In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty shall be increased to five years' imprisonment and fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs fine.

Article 615-3

In addition to the penalties set by Article 615-2 above, the court may order: the publication of its decision at the expense of the convicted person in the newspapers it designates, as well as in any form it deems appropriate; the provisional or permanent closure of the enterprise not approved as a credit institution or Decentralized Financial System (DFS) that has engaged or whose managers have engaged in usurious operations, accompanied by the appointment of an administrator or liquidator; in the case of credit institutions and DFSs, the provisional or permanent closure for the reasons invoked in the second bullet point of this article may only be pronounced after the conforming opinion of the Banking Commission

of UMOA or the Central Bank, under the conditions and according to the procedure provided for by the provisions of the law on banking regulation and that on the regulation of DFSs, in matters of collective procedures for clearing liabilities.
In case of provisional closure, the offender or the enterprise must continue to pay its staff the salaries and allowances of any kind to which they are entitled. This period may not exceed three months.
In case of recidivism, permanent closure is ordered.

Article 615-4

Are liable to the penalties provided for in Article 615-2 above and possibly to the measures set out in Article 615-3 above, those who, responsible in any capacity for the management or administration of an enterprise, company, association, cooperative, or other legal person, knowingly allow any person subject to their authority or control to contravene the provisions of this chapter.

CHAPTER 6: FAMILY IMMUNITIES

Article 616-1

Are not punishable and may only give rise to civil reparations theft, fraud, breach of trust and receiving committed between spouses or by ascendants to the detriment of their children or other descendants.

Article 616-2

Theft, fraud, breach of trust or receiving committed by descendants or between collaterals up to and including the fourth degree or in-laws, may only be prosecuted upon complaint by the injured person.
Withdrawal of the complaint ends the prosecution.

Article 616-3

Persons other than those designated in Articles 616-1 and 616-2 above who have acted as co-perpetrators or accomplices to these offences or who have received the proceeds thereof, may not benefit from the provisions of said articles.

TITLE II

OFFENCES AGAINST IMMOVABLE PROPERTY, DESTRUCTION, DAMAGE AND HARM

CHAPTER 1: OFFENCES AGAINST IMMOVABLE PROPERTY

Article 621-1

Constitutes stellionate the act of appropriating, alienating, selling, exchanging, mortgaging or pledging an immovable property which one knew one did not own or of deliberately presenting it as not encumbered by mortgage or alienable.

Article 621-2

Stellionate is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.
Is deemed a stellionataire: anyone who registers in his name an immovable property which he knows he does not own; anyone who registers a real right on a title which he knows does not belong to him and anyone who knowingly accepts the certificate of registration thus established; anyone who registers an immovable property by knowingly omitting to register the mortgages, real rights or charges with which that immovable property is encumbered; anyone who, knowingly, transfers a title of ownership which he knows does not belong to him and anyone who knowingly accepts this transfer; anyone who, obliged to register a legal mortgage on property subject to registration or a forced mortgage on registered property, grants a conventional mortgage on the property that should have been affected.

Article 621-3

Also constitutes the offence of stellionate and punished by the penalties provided for in Article 621-2 above one of the following acts: the fraudulent gift or fraudulent alienation of an immovable property; the fraudulent organization of the loss or destruction of an urban dwelling permit, a title of ownership or any other provisional or final land title or document; the sale to another, a second or several times, of an immovable property previously sold to a person; the act of selling, exchanging, giving in payment or as security, fraudulently, an inalienable immovable property or one that is the subject of a dispute not yet resolved; the act of selling, exchanging, giving in payment or as security, fraudulently, an immovable property that is the subject of a promise of sale.

Article 621-4

The penalty of imprisonment is from two years to seven years and the fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs, if the perpetrator or accomplice is a registrar, an agent of land services, a magistrate, a lawyer, or a ministerial officer.
The penalty of imprisonment is from two years to ten years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, if the perpetrator or accomplice is a member of government, a deputy, a municipal councillor, a mayor, a prefect, a high commissioner, or a governor.
The attempt of stellionate is punishable.

CHAPTER 2: DESTRUCTION, DAMAGE AND INJURY

Section 1

Destruction, damage and injury other than by incendiary means

Article 622-1

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, whoever wilfully destroys or seriously damages a movable object or immovable property belonging to another.
The penalty of imprisonment is from one year to ten years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, when the destruction or damage is committed with breaking and entering.
The same applies:	when the offence is committed against a magistrate or counsel, with a view to influencing their behaviour in the exercise of or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions;	when the offence is committed against a witness, a victim or any other person, either with a view to inducing them not to report the facts, not to file a complaint, not to give evidence or to give false evidence, or by reason of the report, the complaint or the evidence.

Section 2

Destruction, damage and injury by use of an explosive substance or incendiary means

Article 622-2

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever wilfully destroys or damages a movable object or immovable property belonging to another by means of an explosive or incendiary substance, or a fire or any other means likely to create a danger to the safety of persons.

The penalty of imprisonment is from eleven years to twenty-one years and the fine from one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, if the offence is committed by an organised gang or under the conditions of paragraph 3 of Article 622-1 above.
The penalty is life imprisonment, when the destruction or damage has resulted in the death of a person or an infirmity.

Article 622-3

Is punished by life imprisonment, whoever wilfully destroys or damages a dwelling house, a railway carriage or a vehicle sheltering or containing persons, when the offence is committed by means of an explosive or incendiary substance, a fire or any other means likely to create a danger to the safety of persons and it has resulted in the death of a person.

Article 622-4

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever wilfully sets fire to the bush, the forest, woods, coppices or standing or cut crops.

Article 622-5

The threat to set fire to a dwelling or any other property is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 622-6

Whoever wilfully burns or destroys in any manner registers, minutes or original acts of public authority, titles, notes, bills of exchange, commercial or banking instruments containing or effecting obligations, dispositions or discharge or knowingly destroys, removes, conceals, hides or alters a public or private document likely to facilitate the investigation of crimes or offences, the discovery of evidence or the punishment of their perpetrator, is punished as follows, unless the facts constitute a more serious offence:
a penalty of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) francs to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, if the destroyed documents are acts of public authority or commercial or banking instruments;
a penalty of imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) francs to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, if it concerns any other document.

Section 3: Pillage and devastation

Article 622-7

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any pillage, any damage to foodstuffs or goods, effects, movable property committed in a gathering or gang and by open force.

Article 622-8

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever devastates standing crops or plants grown naturally or by human hand.

Article 622-9

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever commits the offences of pillage provided for in Article 622-7 above when they are committed in time of war.
Is punished by the same penalty any theft committed in premises or in any building abandoned by its occupants, even momentarily or partially destroyed, as a result of events of war.

Section 4: Offences against trees, crops and animals Article 622-10

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever fells without prior authorisation one or more trees in the public domain.
The same penalties are applicable to whoever mutilates, cuts or strips the bark of a tree so as to cause it to perish or destroys its grafts.

Article 622-11

The penalty of imprisonment is from six months to one year and the fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, if the trees were planted in squares, roads, paths, streets or public ways.

Article 622-12

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever cuts grain or fodder that they knew belonged to another.
The imprisonment is from six months to one year and the fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs if green grain was cut.

Article 622-13

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever poisons domestic animals, tamed animals or animals held in captivity or fish in ponds, lakes, rivers, fish ponds or reservoirs.

Article 622-14

Whoever kills without necessity one of the animals mentioned in Article 622-13 above is punished: by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, if the offence was committed in the buildings, enclosures and outbuildings or

on the lands of which the owner of the killed animal was the owner, lessee or tenant farmer; by a term of imprisonment of two months to four months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, if it was committed in places of which the offender was the owner, lessee or tenant farmer; by a term of imprisonment of two months to three months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, if it was committed in any other place.

The maximum penalty is always imposed in case of violation of enclosure.

LIVRE VII

OFFENCES IN COMPUTER MATTERS AND BY MEANS OF INFORMATION AND COMMUNICATION TECHNOLOGIES

Article 700-1

For the purposes of this book, the following are understood as: computer system: any isolated device or set of interconnected or related devices, which ensures or of which one or more elements ensure, in execution of a program, automated processing of data; electronic communication: any transmission to the public or to a category of the public, by an electronic or magnetic communication process, of signs, signals, writings, images, sounds or messages of any nature; computer data: any representation of facts, information or concepts in a form suitable for computer processing, including a program capable of causing a computer system to perform a function; subscriber data: any information, contained in the form of computer data or in any other form, held by a service provider and relating to subscribers to its services,

other than traffic or content data, and making it possible to establish:
•	the type of communication service used, the technical arrangements made in this regard and the period of service;
•	the identity, postal or geographical address and telephone number of the subscriber, and any other access number, billing and payment data, available on the basis of a contract or service arrangement;
•	any other information relating to the location of communication equipment, available on the basis of a contract or service arrangement. traffic data: all data relating to a communication passing through a computer system, produced by the latter as part of the communication chain, indicating the origin, destination, route, time, date, size and duration of the communication or the type of underlying service. service provider: any public or private entity that offers users of its services the possibility of communicating by means of a computer system; any other entity processing or storing computer data for this communication service or its users; information and communication technologies (ICT): technologies used to collect, store, use and transmit information as well as those involving the use of computers or any communication system including telecommunications.

TITRE I: OFFENCES IN COMPUTER MATTERS

CHAPITRE 1: OFFENCES RELATING TO COMPUTER SYSTEMS AND DATA

Article 711-1

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally and without right accesses all or part of a computer system.
When this results in either the deletion, modification or alteration of data contained in the system or an alteration of the functioning of this system, the imprisonment is from one year to five years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 711-2

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) francs, whoever intentionally and without right remains in all or part of a computer system.
When this results in either the deletion, modification or alteration of data contained in the system or an alteration of the functioning of this system, the imprisonment is from two months to three years and the fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 711-3

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally and without right, hinders or distorts the functioning of a computer system, by the introduction, transmission, damaging, erasure, deterioration, alteration and deletion of computer data.

Article 711-4

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally and without right, introduces directly or indirectly computer data into a computer system.

Article 711-5

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally and without right, intercepts, by technical means, computer data, during non-public transmissions, to, from or within a computer system, including electromagnetic emissions from a computer system carrying such computer data.

Article 711-6

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally and without right, damages, erases, deteriorates, alters, modifies, or deletes computer data.

Article 711-7

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally and without right, introduces, alters, modifies, erases or deletes computer data, generating non-authentic data, with the intention that they be taken into account or used for legal purposes as if they were authentic, whether or not they are directly readable and intelligible.

Article 711-8

Is punished by the same penalties whoever, intentionally and without right, makes use of data obtained under the conditions provided for in Article 711-7 above.

Article 711-9

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, intentionally and without right, causes patrimonial harm to another by the introduction, alteration, modification, erasure or deletion of computer data or by any form of interference with the functioning of a computer system, with the fraudulent or criminal intention of obtaining without right an economic benefit for oneself or for another.

Article 711-10

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, intentionally and without right, produces, sells, obtains for use, imports, distributes or makes available in any form:	a device, including a computer program, primarily designed or adapted to enable the commission of one of the offences provided for by Articles 711-1 to 711-6 above.	a password, an access code or similar computer data enabling access to all or part of a computer system with the intention that they be used to commit one or other of the offences provided for by Articles 711-1 to 711-6 above.
Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, intentionally and without right, possesses a device, password, access code or similar data referred to in the first paragraph of this article with the intention that it be used to commit one or other of the offences referred to in Articles 711-1 to 711-6 above.
The offences provided for by this article are not established when the production, sale, obtaining for use, importation, distribution or other forms of making available is not for the purpose of committing an offence, such as in the case of authorised testing or protection of a computer system.

Article 711-11

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, intentionally and without right, participates in an association formed or an agreement established with a view to preparing or committing one or more offences provided for by this title.

Article 711-12

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, intentionally and without right, assumes the digital identity of a third party or makes use of one or more data of any nature enabling their identification, with a view to disturbing their peace, harming their honour, their private life, their property or that of a third party.

Article 711-13

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to four million (4,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, intentionally and without right, intentionally reproduces, extracts or copies computer data belonging to another person.

CHAPITRE 2

BREACHES OF LEGAL OBLIGATIONS IN THE IMPLEMENTATION AND USE OF AN AUTOMATED PERSONAL DATA PROCESSING SYSTEM

Article 712-1

Personal data means any information which enables, in any form whatsoever, directly or not, the identification of natural persons, in particular by reference to an identification number or to one or more elements specific to their physical, physiological, genetic, mental, cultural, social or economic identity.

Article 712-2

Personal data processing means any operation or set of operations carried out using automated or non-automated processes by a natural or legal person, and applied to personal data, such as collection, exploitation, recording, organisation, storage, adaptation, modification, extraction, backup, copying, consultation, use, communication by transmission, dissemination or any other form of making available, comparison or interconnection, as well as locking, encryption, erasure or destruction of personal data.

Article 712-3

The processing controller is the natural or legal person, public or private, who has the power to decide on the creation of personal data.
The recipient of personal data processing is any natural or legal person, public or private, other than the data subject, authorised to receive communication of such data.
The data subject is the identifiable person to whom the personal data relates.

Article 712-4

The act of carrying out or having carried out automated processing of nominative information without the formalities prior to their implementation provided for by law having been complied with is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 712-5

When such information is required by law, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs the act, by the controller of automated processing of personal data:	of not informing the person from whom personal data concerning them is collected:

•	of the identity of the processing controller and, where applicable, that of their representative;
•	of the purpose pursued by the processing for which the data is intended;
•	of the mandatory or optional nature of the responses;
•	of the possible consequences, with regard to them, of a failure to respond;
•	of the recipients or categories of recipients of the data;
•	of their rights of opposition, inquiry, access and rectification;
•	where applicable, of the transfers of personal data envisaged to a foreign State;	when the data is collected by means of a questionnaire, of not including on the questionnaire the information relating to:
•	the identity of the processing controller and, where applicable, that of their representative;
•	the purpose pursued by the processing for which the data is intended;
•	the mandatory or optional nature of the responses;
•	the rights of opposition, inquiry, access and rectification of the persons from whom the data is collected;	of not informing in a clear and precise manner any user of electronic communication networks:
•	of the purpose of any action tending to access, by electronic transmission, information stored in their terminal connection equipment or to record, by the same means, information in their terminal connection equipment;
•	of the means at their disposal to oppose it;
of not providing to the data subject, when the personal data has not been collected from them, the information listed in bullets 1 and 2 from the time of recording the data or, if communication of the data to third parties is envisaged, at the latest at the time of the first communication of the data.

Article 712-6

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, the act of communicating to unauthorised third parties or accessing without authorisation or in an unlawful manner the personal data of another person.

Article 712-7

Is punished by the penalties provided for the offence itself or for the most severely punished offence, anyone who participates in an association formed or an agreement established with a view to the preparation, materialised by one or more material acts, of one or more offences provided for by the above articles of this chapter.

Article 712-8

The act of carrying out or having carried out automated processing of personal information without taking all useful precautions to preserve the security of said information, in particular to prevent it from being distorted, damaged or communicated to unauthorized third parties, is punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 712-9

The misappropriation of purpose of a collection or processing of personal data is punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 712-10

The act of collecting data by fraudulent, unfair or unlawful means, or of carrying out processing of personal information concerning a natural person despite his or her opposition, where such opposition is based on legitimate reasons, is punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.
In the case of automated processing of personal data for the purpose of research in the field of health, the act of carrying out data processing is punishable by the same penalties: without having previously informed the persons concerned individually of their right of access, rectification and opposition, of the nature of the information transmitted and of the recipients of the data; despite the opposition of the person concerned or, where provided for by law, in the absence of the informed and express consent of the person, or, in the case of a deceased person, despite the refusal expressed by that person during his or her lifetime.

Article 712-11

Except in cases provided for by law, the act of storing or keeping in computerized memory, without the express consent of the person concerned, personal data which, directly or indirectly, reveal racial or ethnic origins or political, philosophical or religious opinions or trade union affiliations or the morals of persons, is punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.
The act, except in cases provided for by law, of storing or keeping in computerized memory personal information concerning offences, convictions or security measures is punishable by the same penalties.

Article 712-12

The act, without the agreement of the Commission on Information Technology and Freedoms, of keeping information in personal form beyond the period provided for in the request for an opinion or in the prior declaration to the implementation of

computerized processing is punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 712-13

The act, by any person who has collected, on the occasion of their recording, classification, transmission or another form of processing, personal information the disclosure of which would have the effect of damaging the honor and reputation of the person concerned or the intimacy of his or her private life, of bringing, without authorization from the person concerned, such information to the knowledge of a third party who is not qualified to receive it, is punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.
The disclosure provided for in paragraph 1 is punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs where it was committed through carelessness or negligence.
In the cases provided for in the two preceding paragraphs, prosecution may only be brought upon complaint by the victim, his or her legal representative or his or her successors in title.

Article 712-14

The act of obstructing the action of the Commission is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, either: by opposing on-site verifications; by refusing to communicate to its members or agents the information and documents useful to the mission entrusted to them or by concealing or causing said documents to disappear; by communicating information that does not conform to the content of the records at the time the request was made or that does not present them in a directly intelligible form.

Article 712-15

The provisions of articles 712-6 and 712-8 to 712-13 are applicable to non-automated or mechanographic files whose use does not fall exclusively within the exercise of the right to private life.

TITRE II

OFFENCES COMMITTED BY MEANS OF INFORMATION AND COMMUNICATION TECHNOLOGIES

CHAPITRE 1

OFFENCES BY MEANS OF INFORMATION AND COMMUNICATION TECHNOLOGIES

Article 721-1

Any person who, participating in the execution of an electronic communications service, violates the secrecy of correspondence or who, without the authorization of the operator or the recipient, discloses to an unauthorized third party, publishes or uses the content of said correspondence, is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 721-2

Any person who knowingly uses services obtained by means of the offence referred to in article 721-1 above is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs.

Article 721-3

Any person who fraudulently uses, for personal purposes or otherwise, a network or an electronic communications service open to the public or connects by any means to a private line is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs.

Article 721-4

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who: establishes or causes to be established a network open to the public or an independent network, provides or causes to be provided an electronic communications service in violation of the law or maintains it in violation of a suspension or withdrawal decision; performs or causes to be performed a service subject to individual license or general authorization, without authorization or without license or in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw the license or authorization provided for this purpose by law; performs or causes to be performed a service subject to approval, without approval or in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw the approval provided for this purpose; uses a frequency that has not been previously assigned to them by the regulatory authority; fraudulently uses "call-back" or means of bypassing electronic communications networks open to the public for commercial or personal purposes.
Bypassing within the meaning of the preceding paragraph covers any arrangement or installation allowing a user to have access to long-distance, international or other services, without using the networks of local operators and thus avoiding payment of access charges and, more generally, any means allowing a user not to use the service or facilities of operators holding an authorization.
Call-back means the procedure for establishing telephone communications between correspondents, by which the person requesting the communication is called back by their correspondent.
The court seized may, at the request of the regulatory authority, order the confiscation of installations, apparatus or means of transmission used without authorization or their destruction at the expense of the offender.

Article 721-5

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any person who knowingly transmits or broadcasts false or misleading radioelectric signals or distress and safety calls.

Article 721-6

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any person who carries out radioelectric transmissions knowingly using a call sign from the international series assigned to a State station or a private station.

Article 721-7

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who voluntarily causes, by any means, the interruption of electronic communications.
When the interruption of electronic communications occurs following an act committed voluntarily but without intent to interrupt electronic communications, the perpetrator of the act is punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 721-8

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of fifty million (50,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who knowingly commits an act of theft or vandalism on electronic communications infrastructure.

Article 721-9

Are punishable by a term of imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, offenses relating to private or administrative easements.

Article 721-10

The attempt of the offenses provided for by this title is punishable.

CHAPITRE 2 : DES PEINES COMPLEMENTAIRES

Article 722-1

In case of conviction for an offense committed through an electronic communication means, the competent court may order the confiscation of materials, equipment, instruments, computer programs or data resulting from the offense.
The court may also order as additional penalties the prohibition from sending electronic communication messages, the prohibition on a provisional or permanent basis from accessing the site used to commit the offense or the prohibition from hosting the site by all available technical means.
The judge may issue an injunction to any person legally responsible for the site used to commit the offense, to any qualified person to implement the necessary technical means to guarantee the prohibition of access to or hosting of the incriminated site.
The violation of the prohibitions ordered is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.
In case of conviction for an offense committed through an electronic communication means, the judge orders as an additional measure the dissemination at the expense of the convicted person, by extract, of the decision on that same electronic communication means.
The publication provided for in the preceding paragraph is executed within fifteen days following the day on which the conviction became final.

The convicted person who has not disseminated or caused to be disseminated the extract of the conviction decision within the time limit set in the preceding paragraph, is punishable by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

LIVRE VIII

DES INFRACTIONS PREVUES PAR LES ACTES UNIFORMES DE L'ORGANISATION POUR L'HARMONISATION EN AFRIQUE DU DROIT DES AFFAIRES (OHADA)

TITRE I : DES DISPOSITIONS GENERALES ET PEINES APPLICABLES

CHAPITRE 1 : DES DISPOSITIONS GENERALES

Article 811-1

This title sets the penalties applicable to offenses provided for within the framework of the Organization for the Harmonization of Business Law in Africa (OHADA) by the Uniform Acts: relating to general commercial law; relating to the organization of security interests; relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups; relating to the organization of collective proceedings for the settlement of liabilities; relating to the organization of simplified recovery procedures and enforcement methods; relating to the organization and harmonization of company accounting; relating to the law of cooperative societies.

CHAPITRE 2 : DES PEINES APPLICABLES

Section 1

Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte uniforme relatif au droit commercial général

Article 812-1

In application of Article 69 of the Uniform Act relating to general commercial law, is punishable by a term of imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any person required to complete one of the formalities prescribed

in the Uniform Act and who refrains from doing so, or who carries out a formality by fraud.
If necessary, the court pronouncing the conviction orders the registration or rectification of inaccurate entries.

Article 812-2

In application of Article 140 of the Uniform Act relating to general commercial law, is punishable by the penalties provided for in Article 812-1 above the lessee-manager who does not indicate at the head of their order forms, invoices and other documents of a financial or commercial nature, along with their registration number in the trade and movable credit register, their capacity as lessee-manager of the business.

Section 2

Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte uniforme portant organisation des sûretés

Article 812-3

In application of Article 65 of the Uniform Act on Security Interests, any person who registers a security interest either by fraud or by providing inaccurate information or information given in bad faith is punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.
If necessary, the court pronouncing the conviction orders the registration or rectification of inaccurate entries.

Article 812-4

In application of Article 184 of the Uniform Act on Security Interests, the lessee or any person who, by fraudulent maneuvers, totally or partially deprives the lessor of his privilege is punishable by imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Section 3

Penalties applicable to offenses provided for by the uniform act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups

Paragraphe 1

Penalties applicable to offenses relating to the formation of companies

Article 812-5

In application of Article 886 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, the founders, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the general manager or the deputy general manager of a public limited company who issue shares before registration or at any time when registration is obtained by fraud or when the company is irregularly formed are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-6

In application of Article 887 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, those who are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs:	knowingly, by establishing the notarized declaration of subscription and payment or the certificate of the depositary, have affirmed as sincere and true subscriptions that they knew to be fictitious or have declared that funds which have not been definitively placed at the disposal of the company have been actually paid;	have delivered to the notary or to the depositary, a list of shareholders or subscription and payment forms mentioning fictitious subscriptions or payments of funds which have not been definitively placed at the disposal of the company;	knowingly, by simulation of subscription or payment or by publication of payments which do not exist or of any other false facts, have obtained or attempted to obtain subscriptions or payments;
knowingly, to induce subscriptions or payments, have published the names of persons designated contrary to the truth as being or to be attached to the company in any capacity whatsoever;	fraudulently, have caused a contribution in kind to be assigned a valuation higher than its actual value.

Article 812-7

In application of Article 888 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, those who knowingly negotiate are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs:	registered shares which have not remained in registered form until their full payment;	shares issued for contributions in kind before the expiration of the period during which they are not negotiable;	cash shares for which payment of one quarter of the nominal value has not been made.

Paragraphe 2

Penalties applicable to offenses relating to the management, administration and direction of companies

Article 812-8

In application of Article 889 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, company directors who, in the absence of an inventory or by means of fraudulent inventories, knowingly distribute fictitious dividends among shareholders or members are punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-9

In application of Article 890 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, company directors who knowingly, even in the absence of any distribution of dividends, publish or present to shareholders or members, in order to conceal the true situation of the company, summary financial statements which do not give, for each financial year, a true and fair view of the operations of the financial year, the financial situation and that of the company's assets at the end of that period are punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-10

In application of Article 891 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, the manager of a limited liability company, the directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the general manager or the deputy general manager who, in bad faith, make use of the company's assets or credit in a manner which they know to be contrary to the interests of the company, for personal purposes, material or moral, or to favor another legal entity in which they have an interest, directly or indirectly, are punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Paragraphe 3

Penalties applicable to offenses relating to general meetings

Article 812-11

In application of Article 892 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, those who knowingly prevent a shareholder or member from participating in a general meeting are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Paragraphe 4

Penalties applicable to offenses relating to changes in the capital of public limited companies

Article 812-12

In application of Article 893 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, the directors, the chairman of the board of directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the general manager or the deputy general manager of a public limited company who, during a capital increase, issue shares or share certificates are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs: before the depositary's certificate is established; without the formalities prior to the capital increase being duly completed; without the previously subscribed capital of the company being fully paid up; without the new shares contributed in kind being fully paid up before the amendment registration in the trade and movable credit register; without the new shares being paid up by at least one quarter of their nominal value at the time of subscription; where applicable, without the entire share premium being paid up at the time of subscription.
The persons referred to in this article who do not maintain cash shares in registered form until they are fully paid up are punishable by the same penalties.

Article 812-13

In application of Article 894 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, company directors who, during a capital increase, are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs: do not grant shareholders, in proportion to the amount of their shares, a preferential right to subscribe for cash shares when this right has not been removed by the general meeting and the shareholders have not waived it; do not reserve for shareholders a period of at least twenty days from the opening of the subscription, except when this period is closed early; do not allocate shares made available, for lack of a sufficient number of subscriptions on a non-reducible basis, to shareholders who have subscribed on a reducible basis for a number of shares greater than that which they could subscribe for on a non-reducible basis, in proportion to the rights they hold; do not reserve the rights of holders of subscription warrants.

Article 812-14

In application of Article 895 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, company directors who knowingly give or confirm inaccurate information in the reports presented to the general meeting called to decide on the removal of the preferential subscription right are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-15

In application of Article 896 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, the directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the deputy general manager who knowingly proceed with a capital reduction are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs: without respecting the equality of shareholders; without communicating the capital reduction project to the statutory auditors forty-five days before the holding of the general meeting called to rule on the capital reduction.

Paragraphe 5

Penalties applicable to offences relating to the control of companies

Article 812-16

In application of Article 897 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, company directors who do not initiate the appointment of the company's statutory auditors or do not summon them to general meetings are punishable by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-17

In application of Article 898 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, any person who, either in their own name or as a partner of a statutory auditors' firm, knowingly accepts, exercises or retains the functions of statutory auditor, notwithstanding legal incompatibilities, is punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-18

In application of Article 899 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, any statutory auditor who knowingly, either in their own name or as a partner of a statutory auditors' firm, gives or confirms false information

about the situation of the company or who does not disclose to the public prosecutor criminal acts of which they have knowledge is punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-19

In application of Article 900 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, company directors or any person in the service of the company who knowingly obstruct the verifications or control of the statutory auditors or who refuse the communication, on site, of all documents useful for the exercise of their mission and in particular of all contracts, books, accounting documents and minutes registers are punishable by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Paragraphe 6

Penalties applicable to offences relating to the dissolution of companies

Article 812-20

In application of Article 901 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, company directors who knowingly, when the company's equity becomes less than half of the share capital due to losses recorded in the summary financial statements, are punishable by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs: do not convene, within four months following the approval of the financial statements that revealed these losses, the extraordinary general meeting for the purpose of deciding, if necessary, on the early dissolution of the company; do not file with the registry of the court responsible for commercial matters, do not have registered in the trade and movable credit register and do not publish, in a newspaper authorized to receive legal notices, the early dissolution of the company.

Paragraphe 7

Penalties applicable to offences relating to the liquidation of companies

Article 812-21

In application of Article 902 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, the liquidator of a company who knowingly commits the following acts shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs: within one month from his appointment, does not publish in a journal authorized to receive legal notices of the place of the registered office, the act appointing him liquidator and does not file with the trade and movable credit register the decisions pronouncing the dissolution; does not convene the members, at the end of the liquidation, to rule on the final account of the liquidation, on the discharge of his management and the release of his mandate and to note the closure of the liquidation; in the case provided for by Article 219 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, does not file his final accounts with the registry of the court responsible for commercial matters of the place of the registered office, nor does he request judicial approval thereof.

Article 812-22

In application of Article 903 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, where the liquidation occurs by judicial decision, the liquidator who knowingly commits the following acts shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs: within six months of his appointment, does not present a report on the assets and liabilities of the company in liquidation, and on the continuation of liquidation operations, nor does he request the necessary authorizations to complete them; within three months of the close of each financial year, does not prepare the summary financial statements in view of the inventory and a written report in which

he accounts for the liquidation operations during the past financial year; does not allow the members to exercise, during the liquidation period, their right to access company documents under the same conditions as previously; does not convene the members, at least once a year, to report to them on the summary financial statements in case of continuation of the company's operations; does not deposit in an account opened in a bank in the name of the company in liquidation, within fifteen days from the distribution decision, the sums allocated to distributions among the members and creditors; does not deposit, in a consignment account opened in the books of the Treasury, within one year from the closure of the liquidation, the sums allocated to creditors or members and not claimed by them.

Article 812-23

In application of Article 904 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, the liquidator who, in bad faith, commits the following acts shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs: makes use of the assets or credit of the company in liquidation in a manner he knows to be contrary to the interest of the company, for personal purposes or to favor another legal entity in which he has an interest, directly or indirectly; transfers all or part of the assets of the company in liquidation to a person who held in the company the capacity of general partner, limited partner, manager, member of the board of directors, general administrator or auditor, without having obtained the unanimous consent of the members or, failing that, the authorization of the competent court.

Paragraphe 8 : Des peines applicables aux infractions en cas d'appel public à l'épargne

Article 812-24

In application of Article 905 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups, the presidents, directors or general managers of companies that issue securities offered to the public shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs: without inserting a notice in a journal authorized to receive legal notices, prior to any publicity measure; without the prospectuses and circulars reproducing the statements of the notice provided for in the paragraph above and containing the mention of the insertion of this notice in the journal authorized to receive legal notices with reference to the issue in which it is published; without the posters and advertisements in the newspapers reproducing the same statements or at least, an extract of these statements with reference to said notice, and indications of the issue number of the journal authorized to receive legal notices in which it is published; without the posters, prospectuses and circulars mentioning the signature of the person or representative of the company from which the offer emanates and specifying whether the securities offered are listed or not and, if so, on which stock exchange.
The persons who serve as intermediaries on the occasion of the transfer of securities, without the requirements set out in this article having been complied with, shall be punished by the penalties provided for in paragraph 1.

Section 4

Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte uniforme portant organisation des procédures collectives d'apurement du passif

Paragraphe 1

Des peines applicables à la banqueroute simple et à la banqueroute frauduleuse

Article 812-25

In application of Article 228 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing liabilities, any natural person in a state of cessation of payments who commits the following acts shall be guilty of simple bankruptcy and punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs: enters into commitments deemed too large in view of his situation when he enters into them, without receiving values in exchange; with the intention of delaying the finding of the cessation of his payments, makes purchases with a view to resale below the market price or, with the same intention, uses ruinous means to obtain funds; without legitimate excuse, does not make to the registry of the competent court, the declaration of his state of cessation of payments within thirty days; keeps incomplete or irregular accounts or does not keep them in accordance with accounting rules and recognized practices of the profession having regard to the size of the business; having been declared twice in a state of cessation of payments within a period of five years, these proceedings are closed for insufficiency of assets.

Article 812-26

In application of Article 229 of the Uniform Act on collective proceedings for clearing debts, is guilty of fraudulent bankruptcy and punished by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, any natural person who, in case of cessation of payments: conceals their accounting records; misappropriates or dissipates all or part of their assets; fraudulently acknowledges being a debtor of sums they did not owe either in their records, or by published acts or private written commitments, or in their balance sheet; exercises the commercial profession contrary to a prohibition provided for by the Uniform Acts or by law; after cessation of payments, pays a creditor to the detriment of the estate; stipulates with a creditor particular advantages by reason of their vote in the deliberations of the estate, or makes with a creditor a particular agreement from which results for the latter, an advantage at the expense of the debtor's assets from the day of the opening decision.
Is punished by the penalties provided for fraudulent bankruptcy, any natural person who, on the occasion of a judicial settlement procedure: in bad faith, presents or causes to be presented a profit and loss account, a balance sheet, a statement of receivables or debts or an active and passive statement of privileges and securities, that is inaccurate or incomplete; without authorization from the president of the competent court, accomplishes one of the acts prohibited by Article 11 of the Uniform Act organizing collective proceedings for clearing debts.

Article 812-27
