In application of Article 227 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing debts, the penalties provided for in Articles 812-25 and 812-26 above are applicable to partners of commercial companies who have the status of merchants.:

Article 812-28

In application of Article 230 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing debts, the penalties provided for in Articles 812-29, 812-30 and 812-31 below are applicable to natural persons who are directors of legal persons subject to collective proceedings and to their permanent representatives.

The directors referred to in this article mean all directors de jure or de facto and in a general manner, all persons having directly or through an intermediary, administered, managed or liquidated the legal person under the cover or in place of its legal representatives.

Article 812-29

In application of Article 231 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing debts, are punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the directors referred to in Article 812-28 above who, in that capacity and in bad faith:
consume sums belonging to the legal person by making purely speculative transactions or fictitious transactions; make purchases with a view to resale below market price or employ ruinous means to procure funds, with the intention of delaying the finding of cessation of payments of the legal person; pay or cause to be paid a creditor to the detriment of the estate after cessation of payments of the legal person;
cause the legal person to contract on behalf of others, without it receiving values in exchange, commitments deemed too important having regard to its situation when these were contracted; keep, cause to be kept or allow to be kept irregularly or incompletely the accounting of the legal person under the conditions provided for in Article 228-4 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing debts; fail to make at the registry of the competent court, within the period of thirty days, the declaration of the state of cessation of payments of the legal person; misappropriate or conceal, attempt to misappropriate or conceal a part of their assets or fraudulently acknowledge being debtors of sums they do not owe, with a view to removing all or part of their patrimony from the pursuit of the legal person in a state of cessation of payments or from those of the partners or creditors of the legal person.

Article 812-30

In application of Article 232 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing debts, are guilty of simple bankruptcy and punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the legal or de facto representatives of legal persons comprising partners indefinitely and jointly liable for the debts thereof who, without legitimate excuse, do not make at the registry of the competent court, the declaration of the state of cessation of payments within the period of thirty days or if this declaration does not include the list of jointly liable partners with the indication of their names and domiciles.

Article 812-31

In application of Article 233 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing debts, are punished by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the directors referred to in Article 812-28 who fraudulently: conceal the books of the legal person; misappropriate or conceal a part of its assets; acknowledge the legal person as debtor of sums it does not owe, either in the records, or by public acts or commitments under private signature, or in the balance sheet; exercise the profession of director contrary to a prohibition provided for by the uniform acts or by law; stipulate with a creditor, in the name of the legal person, particular advantages by reason of their vote in the deliberations of the estate or who conclude with a creditor, a particular agreement from which results for the latter, an advantage at the expense of the assets of the legal person, from the day of the decision declaring the cessation of payments.
Are punished by the penalties provided for in paragraph 1, the directors referred to in Article 230 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing debts, who on the occasion of a preventive settlement procedure: in bad faith, present or cause to be presented a profit and loss account, a balance sheet, a statement of receivables or debts or an active and passive statement of privileges and securities, that is inaccurate or incomplete; without authorization from the president of the competent court, accomplish one of the acts prohibited by Article 11 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for clearing debts.

Article 812-32

For the offences provided for in this section, the court seized may pronounce the prohibition from exercising the profession for a duration which may not exceed five years.

Paragraphe 2 : Penalties applicable to offences assimilated to bankruptcy

Article 812-33

In application of Article 240 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, the following are punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs: persons convicted of having, in the interest of the debtor, removed, concealed or hidden all or part of movable or immovable property, without prejudice to the application of criminal provisions relating to complicity; persons convicted of having fraudulently lodged in the collective proceedings, either in their own name or through interposition or impersonation, fictitious claims; persons who, trading under another person's name or under a fictitious name, in bad faith, misappropriate, conceal, attempt to misappropriate or conceal part of their property.

Article 812-34

In application of Article 241 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, the spouse, descendants, ascendants or collaterals of the debtor or his relatives by marriage up to the fourth degree who, without the debtor's knowledge, misappropriate, divert or conceal assets forming part of the debtor's estate in a state of cessation of payments, when the offences are committed to the detriment of an incapacitated person, are punishable by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to nine hundred thousand (900,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-35

In application of Article 242 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, even if there is an acquittal in the cases provided for in Articles 240 and 241 of the said Act, the court seized shall rule on damages and on the reintegration, into the debtor's estate, of property, rights or actions removed.

Article 812-36

In application of Article 243 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, any trustee in collective proceedings who: carries on a personal activity under cover of the debtor's business concealing his actions; disposes of the debtor's credit or property as his own; dissipates the debtor's property; abusively and in bad faith pursues, in his personal interest, either directly or indirectly, a loss-making operation of the debtor's business; acquires for his own account, directly or indirectly, the debtor's property, in violation of Article 51 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, is punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-37

In application of Article 244 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, a creditor who: stipulates with the debtor or with any person, special advantages by reason of his vote in the deliberations of the body of creditors; makes a private agreement from which results in his favour, an advantage at the expense of the debtor's estate from the day of the decision to open the collective proceedings, is punishable by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to nine hundred thousand (900,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-38

In application of the provisions of Article 245 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, the agreements provided for in Article 244 of the said Uniform Act are, in addition, declared null and void by the criminal court, with respect to all persons, even the debtor.
The judgment further orders the creditor to return, to the rightful party, the sums or securities he has received under the annulled agreement.

Article 812-39

In application of Article 246 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, without prejudice to the provisions relating to the criminal record, all conviction decisions rendered pursuant to the provisions of this section shall be, at the expense of those convicted, posted and published in a newspaper authorized to receive legal notices as well as by summary extract, in the Official Gazette mentioning the number of the legal notices newspaper where the first insertion is published.

Section 5

Penalties applicable to offences provided for by the uniform act on simplified recovery and enforcement proceedings

Article 812-40

In application of Article 36 paragraph 1 of the Uniform Act on simplified recovery and enforcement proceedings, the debtor under seizure or the third party holder in whose hands the seizure has been effected who does not produce the seized objects when he is deemed to be the custodian thereof, is punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-41

In application of Articles 64 paragraph 1-6, 100-6 and 109-7 of the Uniform Act on simplified recovery and enforcement proceedings, the debtor or the designated third party who, except in the cases provided for in Article 97 of the Uniform Act

mentioned above, moves or alienates the seized property placed under his custody, is punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-42

In application of Article 128 of the Uniform Act on simplified recovery and enforcement proceedings, the auctioneer or any other auxiliary of justice responsible for the sale who receives a sum above the bid, is punishable by imprisonment of one to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Article 812-43

In application of Article 231 paragraph 1, 5 of the Uniform Act on simplified recovery and enforcement proceedings, the holder who, except in the cases provided for in Article 103 of the said Act, moves or alienates the seized property placed under his custody, is punishable by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Section 6

Penalties applicable to offences provided for by the uniform act on accounting law and financial information

Article 812-44

In application of Article 111 of the Uniform Act on the organization and harmonization of business accounting, individual entrepreneurs and company directors who: for each financial year, do not prepare the inventory and do not establish the annual financial statements as well as, where applicable, the management report and the social balance sheet; knowingly prepare and communicate financial statements that do not present a true and fair view of the assets, financial position and results for the year, are punishable by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

The court seized may, in addition, pronounce a prohibition from exercising the functions of manager of a Limited Liability Company (SARL), Director, Chairman and Chief Executive Officer, Chief Executive Officer, Managing Director or Deputy Managing Director for a period that may not exceed five years.

Section 7

Penalties applicable to offences provided for by the uniform act on the law of cooperative societies

Article 812-45

In application of Article 386 of the Uniform Act on the law of cooperative societies, any person who, without being authorized in accordance with the legal and regulatory provisions applicable to this type of grouping, improperly uses the expressions cooperative societies, union of cooperative societies, federation of cooperative societies or confederation of cooperative societies, accompanied by any qualifier, as well as any denominations likely to suggest that it is one of the groupings cited in this article, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

BOOK IX: MISCELLANEOUS AND FINAL PROVISIONS

Article 900-1

Death sentences pronounced under the former law are automatically commuted to life imprisonment.

Article 900-2

Law No. 043/96/ADP of 13 November 1996 establishing the Penal Code is repealed. The following are also repealed: Articles 736 and 737 of Ordinance 68-7 of 21 February 1968 establishing the Code of Criminal Procedure; Articles 44 to 72, 82 of Law No. 017-99/AN of 29 April 1999 establishing the Drug Code; Articles 1 and 2 of Law No. 007-2004 of 6 April 2004 on the administration of community service work in Burkina Faso; Articles 1 to 5, 13 to 18 of Law No. 26-2008/AN of 8 May 2008 on the repression of acts of vandalism committed during demonstrations on public roads; Articles 1 to 12, 14 to 26 of Law No. 29-2008/AN of 15 May 2008 on combating trafficking in persons and related practices; Articles 1 to 4, 10, 16 to 22 of Law No. 17-2009/AN of 5 May 2009 on the repression of serious banditry; Articles 2 to 12, 14 of Law No. 060-2009/AN of 17 December 2009 on the repression of acts of terrorism in Burkina Faso; Articles 1 to 21 and 23 to 29 of Law No. 11-2014/AN of 17 April 2014 on the repression of the sale of children, child prostitution and child pornography;
Articles 87, 116 paragraph 4 and 121 of Law No. 015-2014/AN of 13 May 2014 on the protection of children in conflict with the law or in danger; Articles 1 to 11, 13, 14 to 16, 25 and 40 to 42 of Law No. 022-2014/AN of 27 May 2014 on the prevention and repression of torture and related practices; Articles 3, 42 to 86 of Law No. 04-2015/CNT of 3 March 2015 on the prevention and repression of corruption in Burkina Faso; Articles 5, 8 to 14, 18 and 19 of Law No. 061-2015/CNT of 6 September 2015 on the prevention, repression and reparation of violence against women and girls and the care of victims; Articles 2, 2 bis, 13, 15 to 15 quinquies of Law No. 084-2015/CNT of 17 December 2015 amending Law No. 060-2009/AN of 17 December 2009 on the repression of acts of terrorism in Burkina Faso.
Finally, all previous provisions to the contrary are repealed.

Article 900-3

This law shall be executed as law of the State.

Thus done and deliberated in public session in Ouagadougou, on 31 May 2018
